import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:get_it/get_it.dart';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:dio_cookie_manager/dio_cookie_manager.dart';
import 'package:cookie_jar/cookie_jar.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';

// --- CORE NETWORK IMPORTS ---
import 'core/network/auth_interceptor.dart';

// --- FEATURE IMPORTS ---

// Search
import 'features/search/domain/repositories/search_repository.dart';
import 'features/search/data/repositories/search_repository_impl.dart';
import 'features/search/data/datasources/search_remote_data_source.dart';
import 'features/search/presentation/bloc/search_bloc.dart';

// Comments
import 'features/comments/data/datasources/comment_remote_data_source.dart';
import 'features/comments/domain/repositories/comment_repository.dart';
import 'features/comments/presentation/bloc/comments_bloc.dart';

// Chat
import 'features/chat/data/repositories/chat_repository.dart';
import 'features/chat/presentation/bloc/chat_bloc.dart';
import 'features/chat/presentation/bloc/notification_bloc.dart';

// Profile
import 'features/profile/data/repositories/profile_repository.dart';
import 'features/profile/presentation/bloc/profile_bloc.dart';

// Auth
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/auth/data/repositories/auth_repository.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/auth/presentation/bloc/auth_bloc.dart';

final sl = GetIt.instance;

Future<void> init() async {
  // =========================================================
  // 1. CORE DEPENDENCIES
  // =========================================================

  final sharedPreferences = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();

  sl.registerLazySingleton<SharedPreferences>(
    () => sharedPreferences,
  );

  sl.registerLazySingleton<LocaleRepository>(
    () => LocaleRepository(sl<SharedPreferences>()),
  );

  // =========================================================
  // 2. SESSION STORAGE AND NETWORK
  // =========================================================

  final appDocDir = await getApplicationDocumentsDirectory();
  final String appDocPath = appDocDir.path;

  final persistCookieJar = PersistCookieJar(
    ignoreExpires: false,
    storage: FileStorage('$appDocPath/.cookies/'),
  );

  sl.registerLazySingleton<CookieJar>(
    () => persistCookieJar,
  );

  sl.registerLazySingleton<Dio>(() {
    final dio = Dio(
      BaseOptions(
        baseUrl: getBaseUrl(),
        connectTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 15),
        receiveTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 15),
        headers: {
          'Accept': 'application/json',
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },

        // Preserve the existing behavior: HTTP 4xx responses are
        // returned as Response objects instead of DioException.
        validateStatus: (status) => status != null && status < 500,
      ),
    );

    // Persistent Rails session cookies.
    dio.interceptors.add(
      CookieManager(sl<CookieJar>()),
    );

    // Automatically attaches the saved Rails auth token.
    dio.interceptors.add(
      AuthInterceptor(
        prefs: sl<SharedPreferences>(),
        onUnauthenticated: () {
          // 401 handling will be implemented in Stage 2.
        },
      ),
    );

    // Log request/response metadata without sensitive headers or bodies.
    dio.interceptors.add(
      LogInterceptor(
        requestHeader: false,
        requestBody: false,
        responseBody: false,
        responseHeader: false,
      ),
    );

    return dio;
  });

  // =========================================================
  // 3. SEARCH
  // =========================================================

  sl.registerFactory(
    () => SearchBloc(
      repository: sl<SearchRepository>(),
      initialLocale: sl<LocaleRepository>().getCachedLocale(),
    ),
  );

  sl.registerLazySingleton<SearchRepository>(
    () => SearchRepositoryImpl(
      remoteDataSource: sl<SearchRemoteDataSource>(),
    ),
  );

  sl.registerLazySingleton<SearchRemoteDataSource>(
    () => SearchRemoteDataSourceImpl(
      client: sl<Dio>(),
    ),
  );

  // =========================================================
  // 4. COMMENTS
  // =========================================================

  sl.registerFactoryParam<CommentsBloc, int, String>(
    (parentId, type) => CommentsBloc(
      repository: sl<CommentRepository>(),
      parentId: parentId,
      type: type,
    ),
  );

  sl.registerLazySingleton<CommentRepository>(
    () => CommentRepository(
      remoteDataSource: sl<CommentRemoteDataSource>(),
    ),
  );

  sl.registerLazySingleton<CommentRemoteDataSource>(
    () => CommentRemoteDataSourceImpl(
      client: sl<Dio>(),
    ),
  );

  // =========================================================
  // 5. CHAT AND NOTIFICATIONS
  // =========================================================

  sl.registerFactory(
    () => ChatBloc(
      repository: sl<ChatRepository>(),
    ),
  );

  sl.registerLazySingleton<NotificationBloc>(
    () => NotificationBloc(
      repository: sl<ChatRepository>(),
    ),
  );

  sl.registerLazySingleton<ChatRepository>(
    () => ChatRepository(
      client: sl<Dio>(),
    ),
  );

  // =========================================================
  // 6. PROFILE
  // =========================================================

  sl.registerFactory(
    () => ProfileBloc(
      repository: sl<ProfileRepository>(),
    ),
  );

  sl.registerLazySingleton<ProfileRepository>(
    () => ProfileRepository(
      client: sl<Dio>(),
    ),
  );

  // =========================================================
  // 7. AUTHENTICATION
  // =========================================================

  sl.registerFactory(
    () => AuthBloc(
      repository: sl<AuthRepository>(),
    ),
  );

  sl.registerLazySingleton<AuthRepository>(
    () => AuthRepository(
      client: sl<Dio>(),
      prefs: sl<SharedPreferences>(),
    ),
  );
}

// ===========================================================
// HELPER FUNCTIONS
// ===========================================================

const bool useRealDevice = false;

String getBaseUrl() {
  if (kIsWeb) {
    return 'http://localhost:3000';
  }

  if (Platform.isAndroid) {
    // Физический телефон подключается к компьютеру по локальному IP.
    return 'http://10.235.162.74:3000';
  }

  return 'http://127.0.0.1:3000';
}
// ===========================================================
// LOCALE REPOSITORY
// ===========================================================

class LocaleRepository {
  final SharedPreferences prefs;

  LocaleRepository(this.prefs);

  String getCachedLocale() {
    return prefs.getString('locale') ?? 'uk';
  }
}

// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/search/data/repositories/search_repository_impl.dart
// LAYER: Data Layer (Repository Implementation)
// RESPONSIBILITY: Concrete implementation routing data sources and preserving
//                 Pagy pagination metadata payload.
// =============================================================================

import '../../domain/repositories/search_repository.dart';
import '../datasources/search_remote_data_source.dart';
import '../models/filter_model.dart';
import '../models/post_detail_model.dart';

class SearchRepositoryImpl implements SearchRepository {
  final SearchRemoteDataSource remoteDataSource;

  SearchRepositoryImpl({required this.remoteDataSource});

  // ===========================================================================
  // 1. GET FILTERS DATA
  // ===========================================================================
  @override
  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> getFiltersData({
    required String category,
    required String locale,
  }) async {
    final Map<String, dynamic> data = await remoteDataSource.getFiltersData(
      category: category,
      locale: locale,
    );

    return {
      'filters': data['filters'] as List<FilterModel>,
      'translations': data['translations'] as Map<String, dynamic>,
    };
  }

  // ===========================================================================
  // 2. SEARCH RECORDS (Returns dynamic payload preserving Pagy metadata)
  // ===========================================================================
  @override
  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> search({
    required String category,
    required Map<String, dynamic> filters,
    required String locale,
  }) async {
    return await remoteDataSource.search(
      category: category,
      filters: filters,
      locale: locale,
    ) as Map<String, dynamic>;
  }

  // ===========================================================================
  // 3. GET POST DETAILS
  // ===========================================================================
  @override
  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> getPostDetails({
    required int id,
    required String category,
    required String locale,
  }) async {
    final Map<String, dynamic> data = await remoteDataSource.getPostDetails(
      id: id,
      category: category,
      locale: locale,
    );

    return {
      'record': data['record'] as PostDetailModel,
      'translations': data['translations'] as Map<String, dynamic>,
    };
  }

  // ===========================================================================
  // 4. CREATE POST (With Geolocation & JSONB Metadata)
  // ===========================================================================
  @override
  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> createPost({
    required String category,
    required String title,
    required String text,
    required int localId,
    required int choiceId,
    int? catId,
    double? latitude,
    double? longitude,
    String? address,
    Map<String, dynamic>? animalMetadata,
    Map<String, dynamic>? thingMetadata,
    required String locale,
    required List<String> imagePaths,
  }) {
    return remoteDataSource.createPost(
      category: category,
      title: title,
      text: text,
      localId: localId,
      choiceId: choiceId,
      catId: catId,
      latitude: latitude,
      longitude: longitude,
      address: address,
      animalMetadata: animalMetadata,
      thingMetadata: thingMetadata,
      locale: locale,
      imagePaths: imagePaths,
    );
  }

  // ===========================================================================
  // 5. UPDATE POST (With Geolocation & JSONB Metadata)
  // ===========================================================================
  @override
  Future<void> updatePost({
    required int postId,
    required String category,
    required String title,
    required String text,
    required int localId,
    required int choiceId,
    int? catId,
    double? latitude,
    double? longitude,
    String? address,
    Map<String, dynamic>? animalMetadata,
    Map<String, dynamic>? thingMetadata,
    required String locale,
    required List<String> existingImages,
    required List<String> newImagePaths,
  }) async {
    return await remoteDataSource.updatePost(
      postId: postId,
      category: category,
      title: title,
      text: text,
      localId: localId,
      choiceId: choiceId,
      catId: catId,
      latitude: latitude,
      longitude: longitude,
      address: address,
      animalMetadata: animalMetadata,
      thingMetadata: thingMetadata,
      locale: locale,
      existingImages: existingImages,
      newImagePaths: newImagePaths,
    );
  }

  // ===========================================================================
  // 6. DELETE POST
  // ===========================================================================
  @override
  Future<void> deletePost(int postId, String category) async {
    return await remoteDataSource.deletePost(postId, category);
  }
}-e 


=== FILE: lib/features/search/data/models/thing_metadata_schema.dart ===
// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/search/data/models/thing_metadata_schema.dart
// RESPONSIBILITY: Схема соответствия подкатегории вещи (Phone slug) 
//                 и списка разрешенных атрибутов идентификации.
// =============================================================================

class ThingMetadataSchema {
  static const Map<String, List<String>> schema = {
    'documents': ['document_type', 'document_number', 'full_name'],
    'phones': ['brand_model', 'imei', 'serial_number', 'color'],
    'cars': ['brand_model', 'vin', 'license_plate', 'color'],
    'motorcycles': ['brand_model', 'vin', 'license_plate', 'color'],
    'bicycles': ['brand_model', 'frame_number', 'color'],
    'jewelry': ['brand_model', 'serial_number', 'hallmark', 'engraving'],
    'keys': ['key_type', 'keychain_description'],
    'bags_wallets': ['brand_model', 'color', 'contents_description'],
    'other': ['brand_model', 'serial_number', 'additional_info'],
  };

  static const Map<String, String> fieldLabels = {
    'vin': 'VIN Code',
    'license_plate': 'License Plate / Reg №',
    'imei': 'IMEI',
    'serial_number': 'Serial Number',
    'frame_number': 'Frame Number',
    'document_type': 'Document Type',
    'document_number': 'Document Number',
    'full_name': 'Full Name',
    'brand_model': 'Brand / Model',
    'color': 'Color',
    'hallmark': 'Hallmark',
    'engraving': 'Engraving',
    'key_type': 'Key Type',
    'keychain_description': 'Keychain Description',
    'contents_description': 'Contents Description',
    'additional_info': 'Additional Info',
  };

  /// Возвращает список полей для категории по её slug/title
  static List<String> getFieldsForCategory(String? categorySlug) {
    if (categorySlug == null) return [];
    final key = categorySlug.toLowerCase().trim();
    return schema[key] ?? schema['other']!;
  }

=== FILE: lib/features/search/data/models/post_detail_model.dart ===
// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/search/data/models/post_detail_model.dart
// LAYER: Data Layer (Model)
// RESPONSIBILITY: Полная модель детального просмотра поста с поддержкой
//                 PostGIS-координат, animal_metadata и thing_metadata (JSONB).
// =============================================================================

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';

// =============================================================================
// 1. КОРНЕВАЯ МОДЕЛЬ ДЕТАЛЬНОГО ПРОСМОТРА (PostDetailModel)
// =============================================================================
class PostDetailModel extends Equatable {
  final int id;
  final String title;
  final String text;
  final String? category; // 'people', 'animals', 'things'
  final String createdAt;
  final String local;
  
  // 👉 Геолокация и адрес (Необходимы для ShowMapWidget)
  final String address;
  final double? lat;
  final double? lng;
  
  // 👉 Специфика животных (JSONB: chip_number, passport_number, insurance_policy_id)
  final Map<String, dynamic> animalMetadata;

  // 👉 Специфика вещей (JSONB: brand_model, vin, license_plate, imei, serial_number, etc.)
  final Map<String, dynamic> thingMetadata;
  
  // 👉 Вложенные сущности и медиа
  final List<String> images;
  final AuthorModel author;
  final List<CommentModel> comments;
  final PermissionsModel permissions;

  const PostDetailModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.title,
    required this.text,
    this.category,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.local,
    this.address = '',
    this.lat,
    this.lng,
    this.animalMetadata = const {},
    this.thingMetadata = const {},
    required this.images,
    required this.author,
    required this.comments,
    required this.permissions,
  });

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Фабрика парсинга JSON
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  factory PostDetailModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    // 1. Парсинг широты (lat / latitude)
    final double? parsedLat = json['lat'] != null
        ? double.tryParse(json['lat'].toString())
        : (json['latitude'] != null ? double.tryParse(json['latitude'].toString()) : null);

    // 2. Парсинг долготы (lng / longitude)
    final double? parsedLng = json['lng'] != null
        ? double.tryParse(json['lng'].toString())
        : (json['longitude'] != null ? double.tryParse(json['longitude'].toString()) : null);

    // 3. Извлечение JSONB-поля animal_metadata
    final Map<String, dynamic> parsedAnimalMeta = json['animal_metadata'] is Map
        ? Map<String, dynamic>.from(json['animal_metadata'])
        : {};

    // 4. Извлечение JSONB-поля thing_metadata (с фоллбэком на things_metadata)
    final Map<String, dynamic> parsedThingMeta = json['thing_metadata'] is Map
        ? Map<String, dynamic>.from(json['thing_metadata'])
        : (json['things_metadata'] is Map
            ? Map<String, dynamic>.from(json['things_metadata'])
            : {});

    return PostDetailModel(
      id: json['id'] as int? ?? 0,
      title: json['title']?.toString() ?? '',
      text: json['text']?.toString() ?? '',
      category: json['category']?.toString() ?? json['type']?.toString() ?? json['commentable_type']?.toString(),
      createdAt: json['created_at_formatted']?.toString() ?? json['created_at']?.toString() ?? '',
      local: json['local']?.toString() ?? '',
      address: json['address']?.toString() ?? '',
      lat: parsedLat,
      lng: parsedLng,
      animalMetadata: parsedAnimalMeta,
      thingMetadata: parsedThingMeta,
      images: List<String>.from(json['images'] ?? []),
      author: AuthorModel.fromJson(json['author'] is Map ? json['author'] : {}),
      comments: (json['comments'] as List? ?? [])
          .map((c) => CommentModel.fromJson(c is Map ? Map<String, dynamic>.from(c) : {}))
          .toList(),
      permissions: PermissionsModel.fromJson(json['permissions'] is Map ? json['permissions'] : {}),
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
        id,
        title,
        text,
        category,
        createdAt,
        local,
        address,
        lat,
        lng,
        animalMetadata,
        thingMetadata,
        images,
        author,
        comments,
        permissions,
      ];
}

// =============================================================================
// 2. МОДЕЛЬ АВТОРА ОБЪЯВЛЕНИЯ (AuthorModel)
// =============================================================================
class AuthorModel extends Equatable {
  final int id;
  final String username;
  final String? avatarUrl;

  const AuthorModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.username,
    this.avatarUrl,
  });

  factory AuthorModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return AuthorModel(
      id: json['id'] as int? ?? 0,
      username: json['username']?.toString() ?? 'Guest',
      avatarUrl: json['avatar_url']?.toString(),
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [id, username, avatarUrl];
}

// =============================================================================
// 3. МОДЕЛЬ КОММЕНТАРИЯ (CommentModel)
// =============================================================================
class CommentModel extends Equatable {
  final int id;
  final String username;
  final String? avatarUrl;
  final String body;
  final String createdAt;
  final bool canDelete;

  const CommentModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.username,
    this.avatarUrl,
    required this.body,
    required this.createdAt,
    this.canDelete = false,
  });

  factory CommentModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    final userData = json['user'] is Map ? json['user'] as Map<String, dynamic> : null;
    final authorData = json['author'] is Map ? json['author'] as Map<String, dynamic> : null;

    final String extractedUsername = userData?['username']?.toString() ??
        authorData?['username']?.toString() ??
        'User #${json['user_id'] ?? 0}';

    final String? extractedAvatar = userData?['avatar_url']?.toString() ??
        authorData?['avatar_url']?.toString();

    final bool permissionDelete = json['permissions'] is Map
        ? (json['permissions']['can_delete'] == true)
        : (json['can_delete'] == true);

    return CommentModel(
      id: json['id'] as int? ?? 0,
      username: extractedUsername,
      avatarUrl: extractedAvatar,
      body: json['body']?.toString() ?? '',
      createdAt: json['created_at_formatted']?.toString() ?? json['created_at']?.toString() ?? '',
      canDelete: permissionDelete,
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [id, username, avatarUrl, body, createdAt, canDelete];
}

// =============================================================================
// 4. МОДЕЛЬ ПРАВ ДОСТУПА К ПОСТУ (PermissionsModel)
// =============================================================================
class PermissionsModel extends Equatable {
  final bool canEdit;
  final bool canDelete;

  const PermissionsModel({
    required this.canEdit,
    required this.canDelete,
  });

  factory PermissionsModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return PermissionsModel(
      canEdit: json['can_edit'] == true,
      canDelete: json['can_delete'] == true,
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [canEdit, canDelete];

class FilterOption {
  final int id;      
  final String label; 

  FilterOption({required this.id, required this.label});

  factory FilterOption.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return FilterOption(
      // Приводим к int, даже если Rails пришлет строку (защита)
      id: int.parse(json['id'].toString()),
      label: json['label'] as String? ?? '',
    );
  }
}

class FilterModel {
  final String id;    
  final String type;  
  final String label; 
  final List<FilterOption>? options; 

  FilterModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.type,
    required this.label,
    this.options,
  });

  factory FilterModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return FilterModel(
      id: json['id'] as String? ?? '',
      type: json['type'] as String? ?? 'text',
      label: json['label'] as String? ?? '',
      options: (json['options'] as List<dynamic>?)
          ?.map((e) => FilterOption.fromJson(e as Map<String, dynamic>))
          .toList(),
    );
  }

=== FILE: lib/features/search/data/datasources/search_remote_data_source.dart ===
// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/search/data/datasources/search_remote_data_source.dart
// LAYER: Data Layer (Remote Data Source)
// RESPONSIBILITY: Выполнение HTTP-запросов (Dio) к API Rails с поддержкой
//                 геолокации, animal_metadata, thing_metadata и сохранением Pagy-пагинации.
// =============================================================================

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;

import '../models/filter_model.dart';
import '../models/post_detail_model.dart';

// =============================================================================
// 👉 КОНТРАКТ (ИНТЕРФЕЙС)
// =============================================================================
abstract class SearchRemoteDataSource {
  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> getFiltersData({
    required String category,
    required String locale,
  });

  Future<dynamic> search({
    required String category,
    required Map<String, dynamic> filters,
    required String locale,
  });

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> getPostDetails({
    required int id,
    required String category,
    required String locale,
  });

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> createPost({
    required String category,
    required String title,
    required String text,
    required int localId,
    required int choiceId,
    int? catId,
    
    // 👉 Геолокация и идентификаторы (JSONB)
    double? latitude,
    double? longitude,
    String? address,
    Map<String, dynamic>? animalMetadata,
    Map<String, dynamic>? thingMetadata,
    
    required String locale,
    required List<String> imagePaths,
  });

  Future<void> updatePost({
    required int postId,
    required String category,
    required String title,
    required String text,
    required int localId,
    required int choiceId,
    int? catId,
    
    // 👉 Геолокация и идентификаторы (JSONB)
    double? latitude,
    double? longitude,
    String? address,
    Map<String, dynamic>? animalMetadata,
    Map<String, dynamic>? thingMetadata,
    
    required String locale,
    required List<String> existingImages,
    required List<String> newImagePaths,
  });

  Future<void> deletePost(int postId, String category);
}

// =============================================================================
// 👉 РЕАЛИЗАЦИЯ (ИМПЛЕМЕНТАЦИЯ)
// =============================================================================
class SearchRemoteDataSourceImpl implements SearchRemoteDataSource {
  final Dio client;

  SearchRemoteDataSourceImpl({required this.client});

  @override
  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> getFiltersData({
    required String category,
    required String locale,
  }) async {
    final filtersFuture = client.get(
      '/api/v1/filters',
      queryParameters: {
        'category': category,
        'locale': locale,
      },
    );

    final metaFuture = client.get(
      '/api/v1/search/new_meta',
      queryParameters: {
        'category': category,
        'locale': locale,
      },
    ).catchError((e) {
      return Response(
        requestOptions: RequestOptions(path: ''),
        data: {'translations': {}},
      );
    });

    final results = await Future.wait([filtersFuture, metaFuture]);
    final filtersResponse = results[0];
    final metaResponse = results[1];

    final filtersData = filtersResponse.data;
    final metaData = metaResponse.data;

    if (filtersData is Map<String, dynamic>) {
      final List rawFilters = filtersData['filters'] ?? [];
      final filters = rawFilters.map((json) => FilterModel.fromJson(json)).toList();
      final baseTranslations = filtersData['translations'] is Map 
          ? Map<String, dynamic>.from(filtersData['translations']) 
          : <String, dynamic>{};
      final metaTranslations = (metaData is Map<String, dynamic> && metaData['translations'] is Map)
          ? Map<String, dynamic>.from(metaData['translations'])
          : <String, dynamic>{};

      final combinedTranslations = {
        ...baseTranslations,
        ...metaTranslations,
      };

      return {
        'filters': filters,
        'translations': combinedTranslations,
      };
    }
    throw Exception("Unexpected API response format");
  }

  @override
  Future<dynamic> search({
    required String category,
    required Map<String, dynamic> filters,
    required String locale,
  }) async {
    final Map<String, dynamic> queryParameters = {
      'category': category,
      'locale': locale,
      ...filters,
    };

    if (queryParameters.containsKey('sort') && queryParameters['sort'] is String) {
      final sortVal = queryParameters['sort'] as String;
      if (sortVal.contains('_')) {
        final parts = sortVal.split('_');
        queryParameters['sort_by'] = parts.sublist(0, parts.length - 1).join('_');
        queryParameters['order'] = parts.last;
      }
    }

    final response = await client.get('/api/v1/search', queryParameters: queryParameters);
    return response.data;
  }

  @override
  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> getPostDetails({
    required int id,
    required String category,
    required String locale,
  }) async {
    final response = await client.get(
      '/api/v1/search/$id',
      queryParameters: {'category': category, 'locale': locale},
    );
    final data = response.data;
    if (data is Map<String, dynamic>) {
      final record = PostDetailModel.fromJson(data['record'] ?? {});
      final translations = data['translations'] as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
      return {'record': record, 'translations': translations};
    }
    throw Exception("Unexpected API response format for details");
  }

  @override
  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> createPost({
    required String category,
    required String title,
    required String text,
    required int localId,
    required int choiceId,
    int? catId,
    double? latitude,
    double? longitude,
    String? address,
    Map<String, dynamic>? animalMetadata,
    Map<String, dynamic>? thingMetadata,
    required String locale,
    required List<String> imagePaths,
  }) async {
    final normalizedCategory = category.toLowerCase();
    String? subCategoryKey;
    switch (normalizedCategory) {
      case 'people': subCategoryKey = 'live_id'; break;
      case 'animals': subCategoryKey = 'cat_id'; break;
      case 'things': subCategoryKey = 'phone_id'; break;
    }

    final Map<String, dynamic> data = {
      'category': normalizedCategory,
      'title': title,
      'text': text,
      'local_id': localId,
      'choice_id': choiceId,
      'locale': locale,
    };

    if (catId != null && subCategoryKey != null) {
      data[subCategoryKey] = catId;
    }

    // Добавление координат и метаданных в payload
    if (latitude != null) data['latitude'] = latitude;
    if (longitude != null) data['longitude'] = longitude;
    if (address != null && address.isNotEmpty) data['address'] = address;
    if (animalMetadata != null && animalMetadata.isNotEmpty) {
      data['animal_metadata'] = animalMetadata;
    }
    if (thingMetadata != null && thingMetadata.isNotEmpty) {
      data['thing_metadata'] = thingMetadata;
    }

    final formData = FormData.fromMap(data);
    for (final path in imagePaths) {
      formData.files.add(MapEntry('images[]', await MultipartFile.fromFile(path, filename: p.basename(path))));
    }

    final response = await client.post('/api/v1/posts', data: formData, options: Options(headers: {'Accept': 'application/json'}));
    final resData = response.data;
    if (resData is Map<String, dynamic>) return resData;
    throw Exception("Invalid response format on createPost");
  }

  @override
  Future<void> updatePost({
    required int postId,
    required String category,
    required String title,
    required String text,
    required int localId,
    required int choiceId,
    int? catId,
    double? latitude,
    double? longitude,
    String? address,
    Map<String, dynamic>? animalMetadata,
    Map<String, dynamic>? thingMetadata,
    required String locale,
    required List<String> existingImages,
    required List<String> newImagePaths,
  }) async {
    final normalizedCategory = category.toLowerCase();
    String? subCategoryKey;
    switch (normalizedCategory) {
      case 'people': subCategoryKey = 'live_id'; break;
      case 'animals': subCategoryKey = 'cat_id'; break;
      case 'things': subCategoryKey = 'phone_id'; break;
    }

    final Map<String, dynamic> data = {
      'category': normalizedCategory,
      'title': title,
      'text': text,
      'local_id': localId,
      'choice_id': choiceId,
      'locale': locale,
    };

    if (catId != null && subCategoryKey != null) {
      data[subCategoryKey] = catId;
    }

    // Добавление координат и метаданных в payload
    if (latitude != null) data['latitude'] = latitude;
    if (longitude != null) data['longitude'] = longitude;
    if (address != null && address.isNotEmpty) data['address'] = address;
    if (animalMetadata != null && animalMetadata.isNotEmpty) {
      data['animal_metadata'] = animalMetadata;
    }
    if (thingMetadata != null && thingMetadata.isNotEmpty) {
      data['thing_metadata'] = thingMetadata;
    }

    if (existingImages.isNotEmpty) {
      data['existing_images[]'] = existingImages;
    }

    final formData = FormData.fromMap(data);

    for (final path in newImagePaths) {
      formData.files.add(
        MapEntry(
          'new_images[]',
          await MultipartFile.fromFile(path, filename: p.basename(path))
        )
      );
    }

    final response = await client.patch(
      '/api/v1/posts/$postId',
      data: formData,
      options: Options(headers: {'Accept': 'application/json'}),
    );

    if (response.statusCode != 200 && response.statusCode != 204) {
      throw Exception('Failed to update post');
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> deletePost(int postId, String category) async {
    final response = await client.delete(
      '/api/v1/posts/$postId', 
      queryParameters: {
        'category': category, 
      },
      options: Options(
        headers: {'Accept': 'application/json'},
      ),
    );

    if (response.statusCode != 200 && response.statusCode != 204) {
      throw Exception('Failed to delete post');
    }
  }
}-e

=== FILE: lib/features/search/presentation/widgets/map/location_picker_widget.dart ===

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_map/flutter_map.dart';
import 'package:latlong2/latlong.dart';

class LocationPickerWidget extends StatefulWidget {
  final double initialLat;
  final double initialLng;
  final Map<String, dynamic> translations;
  final Function(double lat, double lng) onLocationSelected;
  final Function(String address) onAddressChanged;

  const LocationPickerWidget({
    super.key,
    this.initialLat = 50.0412, // Дефолтный центр
    this.initialLng = 21.9991,
    required this.translations,
    required this.onLocationSelected,
    required this.onAddressChanged,
  });

  @override
  State<LocationPickerWidget> createState() => _LocationPickerWidgetState();
}

class _LocationPickerWidgetState extends State<LocationPickerWidget> {
  late MapController _mapController;
  late LatLng _currentPosition;
  final TextEditingController _addressController = TextEditingController();

  Timer? _debounce;
  final Dio _geocodingDio = Dio();
  bool _isSearching = false;

  final Color _cardDark = const Color(0xFF1E293B);
  final Color _bgDark = const Color(0xFF0F172A);
  final Color _accentCyan = const Color(0xFF00F2FF);
  final Color _accentOrange = const Color(0xFFFF8A00);

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _mapController = MapController();
    _currentPosition = LatLng(widget.initialLat, widget.initialLng);
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _debounce?.cancel();
    _addressController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  String _tr(String key, String fallback) {
    return (widget.translations[key] ?? widget.translations['map_picker.$key'] ?? fallback).toString();
  }

  // ===========================================================================
  // АКТИВНЫЙ ГЕОКОДИНГ (NOMINATIM / OPENSTREETMAP)
  // ===========================================================================
  void _onAddressTextChanged(String text) {
    // 1. Передаем введенный текст в форму
    widget.onAddressChanged(text);

    // 2. Сбрасываем предыдущий таймер
    if (_debounce?.isActive ?? false) _debounce!.cancel();

    final query = text.trim();
    if (query.length < 3) return;

    // 3. Задержка 600 мс (Debounce) перед запросом к геокодеру
    _debounce = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 600), () async {
      if (!mounted) return;

      setState(() => _isSearching = true);

      try {
        final response = await _geocodingDio.get(
          'https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search',
          queryParameters: {
            'q': query,
            'format': 'json',
            'limit': 1,
          },
          options: Options(
            headers: {
              'User-Agent': 'FindWayMobileApp/1.0', // Требование Nominatim API
            },
          ),
        );

        if (response.data is List && (response.data as List).isNotEmpty) {
          final firstResult = response.data[0];
          final double? searchedLat = double.tryParse(firstResult['lat'].toString());
          final double? searchedLng = double.tryParse(firstResult['lon'].toString());

          if (searchedLat != null && searchedLng != null && mounted) {
            final newPos = LatLng(searchedLat, searchedLng);
            
            setState(() {
              _currentPosition = newPos;
            });

            // Центрируем карту и обновляем маркер
            _mapController.move(newPos, 15.0);
            
            // Передаем точные координаты в родительскую форму
            widget.onLocationSelected(searchedLat, searchedLng);
          }
        }
      } catch (_) {
        // Игнорируем ошибки сети при поиске
      } finally {
        if (mounted) {
          setState(() => _isSearching = false);
        }
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: _cardDark,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: _accentCyan.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
      ),
      child: ClipRRect(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            // Заголовок карточки
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
              color: _bgDark,
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.map_outlined, color: _accentCyan, size: 18),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Text(
                    _tr('location_label', 'Вкажіть місце на карті').toUpperCase(),
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: _accentCyan,
                      fontFamily: 'Orbitron',
                      fontSize: 11,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),

            // Область интерактивной карты
            SizedBox(
              height: 220,
              child: Stack(
                children: [
                  FlutterMap(
                    mapController: _mapController,
                    options: MapOptions(
                      initialCenter: _currentPosition,
                      initialZoom: 14.0,
                      onTap: (_, latLng) {
                        setState(() => _currentPosition = latLng);
                        widget.onLocationSelected(latLng.latitude, latLng.longitude);
                      },
                      onPositionChanged: (pos, hasGesture) {
                        if (hasGesture && pos.center != null) {
                          _currentPosition = pos.center!;
                          widget.onLocationSelected(_currentPosition.latitude, _currentPosition.longitude);
                        }
                      },
                    ),
                    children: [
                      TileLayer(
                        urlTemplate: 'https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png',
                        userAgentPackageName: 'com.findway.findway_mobile',
                      ),
                      MarkerLayer(
                        markers: [
                          Marker(
                            point: _currentPosition,
                            width: 40,
                            height: 40,
                            child: Icon(
                              Icons.location_on,
                              color: _accentOrange,
                              size: 36,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),

                  // Подсказка поверх карты
                  Positioned(
                    top: 8,
                    left: 8,
                    right: 8,
                    child: Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 6),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.75),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        _tr('picker_hint', 'Клацніть на карті або введіть адресу нижче'),
                        style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 10),
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),

            // Инпут текстового поиска адреса с анимацией загрузки
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12.0),
              child: TextFormField(
                controller: _addressController,
                onChanged: _onAddressTextChanged,
                style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 13),
                decoration: InputDecoration(
                  hintText: _tr('adress_placeholder', 'Введіть адресу або орієнтир'),
                  hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white38, fontSize: 12),
                  prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.edit_location_alt, color: _accentCyan, size: 18),
                  suffixIcon: _isSearching
                      ? Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12.0),
                          child: SizedBox(
                            width: 16,
                            height: 16,
                            child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                              strokeWidth: 2,
                              color: _accentCyan,
                            ),
                          ),
                        )
                      : null,
                  filled: true,
                  fillColor: _bgDark,
                  contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
                  border: OutlineInputBorder(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    borderSide: BorderSide.none,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

=== FILE: lib/features/search/presentation/widgets/map/index_map_widget.dart ===
// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/search/presentation/widgets/map/index_map_widget.dart
// LAYER: Presentation Layer
// RESPONSIBILITY: Interactive OpenStreetMap with GPS Location & Radius Filtering.
// =============================================================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_map/flutter_map.dart';
import 'package:geolocator/geolocator.dart';
import 'package:latlong2/latlong.dart';

class IndexMapWidget extends StatefulWidget {
  final List<dynamic> results;
  final Function(int id, String category)? onMarkerTap;
  final Function(LatLng location)? onLocationFound;
  final double? radiusKm;
  final Map<String, dynamic>? translations;

  const IndexMapWidget({
    super.key,
    required this.results,
    this.onMarkerTap,
    this.onLocationFound,
    this.radiusKm,
    this.translations,
  });

  @override
  State<IndexMapWidget> createState() => _IndexMapWidgetState();
}

class _IndexMapWidgetState extends State<IndexMapWidget> {
  bool _isExpanded = false;
  bool _isLocating = false;
  LatLng? _userLocation;
  final MapController _mapController = MapController();

  // ===========================================================================
  // МЕНЕДЖЕР ГЕОЛОКАЦИИ И ПРОВЕРКИ СИСТЕМНОГО GPS
  // ===========================================================================
  Future<void> _getCurrentLocation() async {
    setState(() => _isLocating = true);

    try {
      // 1. Проверяем, включен ли GPS в системе смартфона
      bool serviceEnabled = await Geolocator.isLocationServiceEnabled();
      if (!serviceEnabled) {
        if (!mounted) return;
        _showGpsDisabledDialog();
        setState(() => _isLocating = false);
        return;
      }

      // 2. Проверяем и запрашиваем разрешения
      LocationPermission permission = await Geolocator.checkPermission();
      if (permission == LocationPermission.denied) {
        permission = await Geolocator.requestPermission();
        if (permission == LocationPermission.denied) {
          _showToast("Доступ к геолокации отклонен");
          setState(() => _isLocating = false);
          return;
        }
      }

      if (permission == LocationPermission.deniedForever) {
        _showToast("Геолокация заблокирована в настройках устройства");
        await Geolocator.openAppSettings();
        setState(() => _isLocating = false);
        return;
      }

      // 3. Получаем точные координаты
      Position position = await Geolocator.getCurrentPosition(
        locationSettings: const LocationSettings(
          accuracy: LocationAccuracy.high,
          timeLimit: Duration(seconds: 10),
        ),
      );

      final userLatLng = LatLng(position.latitude, position.longitude);

      setState(() {
        _userLocation = userLatLng;
        _isLocating = false;
      });

      // Перемещаем камеру карты на позицию пользователя
      _mapController.move(userLatLng, 13.0);

      // Уведомляем родительский виджет для поиска по радиусу
      if (widget.onLocationFound != null) {
        widget.onLocationFound!(userLatLng);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("[IndexMapWidget] Location Error: $e");
      _showToast("Не удалось определить местоположение");
      setState(() => _isLocating = false);
    }
  }

  void _showGpsDisabledDialog() {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (dialogContext) => AlertDialog(
        backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF1E293B),
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
        title: const Row(
          children: [
            Icon(Icons.location_off_rounded, color: Color(0xFFFF8A00)),
            SizedBox(width: 10),
            Text("GPS выключен", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 16)),
          ],
        ),
        content: const Text(
          "Для поиска объектов поблизости необходимо включить геолокацию на устройстве.",
          style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 13),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(dialogContext),
            child: const Text("ОТМЕНА", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white54)),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF00F2FF),
              foregroundColor: const Color(0xFF0F172A),
            ),
            onPressed: () {
              Navigator.pop(dialogContext);
              Geolocator.openLocationSettings();
            },
            child: const Text("ВКЛЮЧИТЬ GPS", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showToast(String message) {
    if (!mounted) return;
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: Text(message),
        backgroundColor: Colors.deepOrange,
        behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // 💡 Фильтрация постов: наличие координат + попадание в радиус от пользователя
    final validPosts = widget.results.where((p) {
      if (p is! Map) return false;
      final double? lat = double.tryParse(p['lat']?.toString() ?? '');
      final double? lng = double.tryParse(p['lng']?.toString() ?? '');
      if (lat == null || lng == null) return false;

      // Если геолокация определена и задан радиус — фильтруем по дистанции
      if (_userLocation != null && widget.radiusKm != null && widget.radiusKm! > 0) {
        final double distanceInMeters = Geolocator.distanceBetween(
          _userLocation!.latitude,
          _userLocation!.longitude,
          lat,
          lng,
        );
        final double distanceInKm = distanceInMeters / 1000.0;
        return distanceInKm <= widget.radiusKm!;
      }

      return true;
    }).toList();

    LatLng initialCenter = _userLocation ?? const LatLng(50.0412, 21.9991);
    if (_userLocation == null && validPosts.isNotEmpty) {
      final double? lat = double.tryParse(validPosts.first['lat'].toString());
      final double? lng = double.tryParse(validPosts.first['lng'].toString());
      if (lat != null && lng != null) {
        initialCenter = LatLng(lat, lng);
      }
    }

    final String toggleMapLabel = widget.translations?['toggle_map'] ??
        widget.translations?['map_index.toggle_map'] ??
        'Interactive map Articles';

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        SizedBox(
          width: double.infinity,
          child: ElevatedButton.icon(
            onPressed: () {
              setState(() {
                _isExpanded = !_isExpanded;
              });
              if (_isExpanded && _userLocation == null) {
                _getCurrentLocation();
              }
            },
            icon: const Icon(Icons.map_rounded, color: Color(0xFF0B0F19), size: 18),
            label: Text(
              '🗺️ ${toggleMapLabel.toUpperCase()} (${validPosts.length})',
              style: const TextStyle(
                color: Color(0xFF0B0F19),
                fontFamily: 'Orbitron',
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                fontSize: 11,
                letterSpacing: 0.8,
              ),
            ),
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF00F2FF),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12, horizontal: 16),
              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
              ),
              elevation: 4,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        AnimatedContainer(
          duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
          curve: Curves.easeInOut,
          height: _isExpanded ? 320 : 0,
          margin: EdgeInsets.only(top: _isExpanded ? 12 : 0),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
            border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFF00F2FF).withValues(alpha: 0.4), width: 1.5),
            boxShadow: _isExpanded
                ? [BoxShadow(color: const Color(0xFF00F2FF).withValues(alpha: 0.15), blurRadius: 12)]
                : [],
          ),
          child: ClipRRect(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15),
            child: _isExpanded
                ? Stack(
                    children: [
                      FlutterMap(
                        mapController: _mapController,
                        options: MapOptions(
                          initialCenter: initialCenter,
                          initialZoom: 10.0,
                        ),
                        children: [
                          TileLayer(
                            urlTemplate: 'https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png',
                            userAgentPackageName: 'com.findway.findway_mobile',
                          ),

                          // 1. Слой радиуса вокруг пользователя
                          if (_userLocation != null && widget.radiusKm != null)
                            CircleLayer(
                              circles: [
                                CircleMarker(
                                  point: _userLocation!,
                                  radius: widget.radiusKm! * 1000, // Перевод в метры
                                  useRadiusInMeter: true,
                                  color: const Color(0xFF00F2FF).withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                                  borderColor: const Color(0xFF00F2FF),
                                  borderStrokeWidth: 2,
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),

                          // 2. Слой маркеров постов (только отфильтрованные)
                          MarkerLayer(
                            markers: [
                              ...validPosts.map((post) {
                                final double lat = double.parse(post['lat'].toString());
                                final double lng = double.parse(post['lng'].toString());
                                final LatLng pos = LatLng(lat, lng);

                                return Marker(
                                  point: pos,
                                  width: 40,
                                  height: 40,
                                  child: GestureDetector(
                                    onTap: () {
                                      if (widget.onMarkerTap != null) {
                                        final int id = int.parse(post['id'].toString());
                                        final String category = post['category']?.toString() ?? 'people';
                                        widget.onMarkerTap!(id, category);
                                      }
                                    },
                                    child: Container(
                                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                        color: const Color(0xFF0F172A),
                                        shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                        border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFF00F2FF), width: 2),

                                      child: const Icon(
                                        Icons.location_on,
                                        color: Color(0xFFFF8A00),
                                        size: 22,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                );
                              }),

                              // 3. Маркер текущей позиции пользователя
                              if (_userLocation != null)
                                Marker(
                                  point: _userLocation!,
                                  width: 24,
                                  height: 24,
                                  child: Container(
                                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                      color: const Color(0xFF00F2FF),
                                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                      border: Border.all(color: Colors.white, width: 3),
                                      boxShadow: [
                                        BoxShadow(
                                          color: const Color(0xFF00F2FF).withValues(alpha: 0.6),
                                          blurRadius: 10,
                                          spreadRadius: 2,
                                        ),
                                      ],
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),

                      // Кнопка определения GPS поверх карты
                      Positioned(
                        right: 12,
                        bottom: 12,
                        child: FloatingActionButton.small(
                          heroTag: 'index_map_gps_btn',
                          backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF0F172A),
                          foregroundColor: const Color(0xFF00F2FF),
                          elevation: 4,
                          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                            side: const BorderSide(color: Color(0xFF00F2FF), width: 1),
                          ),
                          onPressed: _isLocating ? null : _getCurrentLocation,
                          child: _isLocating
                              ? const SizedBox(

                                    color: Color(0xFF00F2FF),
                                    strokeWidth: 2,
                                  ),

                              : const Icon(Icons.my_location_rounded, size: 20),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  )
                : const SizedBox.shrink(),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_map/flutter_map.dart';
import 'package:latlong2/latlong.dart';

class ShowMapWidget extends StatelessWidget {

  final double lng;

  final String address;

  const ShowMapWidget({
    super.key,

    required this.lng,
    required this.title,
    required this.address,
    this.translations,

  @override

// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/search/presentation/widgets/articles_card.dart
// LAYER: Presentation Layer (Widget)
// RESPONSIBILITY: Renders interactive post card with image carousel, status badges,
//                 and detail navigation.
// =============================================================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import '../bloc/search_bloc.dart';
import '../bloc/search_event.dart';
import '../pages/post_card_page.dart';

class ArticleCard extends StatefulWidget {
  final dynamic post;
  final String currentLocale;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;

  const ArticleCard({
    required this.post, 
    required this.currentLocale, 
    this.onTap,
    super.key,
  });

  @override
  State<ArticleCard> createState() => _ArticleCardState();
}

class _ArticleCardState extends State<ArticleCard> {
  late PageController _pageController;
  int _currentPage = 0;
  List<String> _images = [];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _pageController = PageController();
    _parseImages();
  }

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant ArticleCard oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    if (oldWidget.post != widget.post) {
      _parseImages();
    }
  }

  void _parseImages() {
    final List<String> temp = [];
    final dynamic imagesData = _getField('images_urls') ?? _getField('images') ?? _getField('photos');

    if (imagesData is List && imagesData.isNotEmpty) {
      temp.addAll(imagesData.map((e) => e.toString()));
    } else {
      final singleImg = _getField('image_url');
      if (singleImg != null && singleImg.toString().isNotEmpty) {
        temp.add(singleImg.toString());
      }
    }

    if (temp.isEmpty) {
      final String cat = _getField('category')?.toString().toLowerCase() ?? '';
      
      switch (cat) {
        case 'animals':
        case 'sense':
          temp.add('assets/images/cat.png');
          break;
        case 'things':
        case 'thing':
          temp.add('assets/images/things.png');
          break;
        case 'people':
        case 'article':
        default:
          temp.add('assets/images/peop.png');
          break;
      }
    }

    setState(() => _images = temp);
  }

  dynamic _getField(String key) {
    if (widget.post == null) return null;
    if (widget.post is Map) return widget.post[key];
    try {
      return (widget.post as dynamic).toJson()[key];
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  int _getPostId() {
    final rawId = _getField('id');
    if (rawId is int) return rawId;
    return int.tryParse(rawId?.toString() ?? '0') ?? 0;
  }

  String _getCategory() {
    return _getField('category')?.toString() ?? 'people';
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _pageController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final String statusLabel = (_getField('choice_label') ?? '').toString().toUpperCase();
    final String title = (_getField('title') ?? '').toString();

    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.1), 
            blurRadius: 10, 
            offset: const Offset(0, 5),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: ClipRRect(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        child: Stack(
          children: [
            // 1. ИЗОБРАЖЕНИЯ / ПЕРЕЛИСТЫВАНИЕ
            Positioned.fill(
              child: PageView.builder(
                controller: _pageController,
                itemCount: _images.length,
                onPageChanged: (i) => setState(() => _currentPage = i),
                itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                  final imgPath = _images[index];
                  return GestureDetector(
                    onTap: _navigateToDetails,
                    child: imgPath.startsWith('http')
                        ? Image.network(
                            imgPath,
                            fit: BoxFit.cover,
                            errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) => Image.asset('assets/images/peop.png', fit: BoxFit.cover),
                          )
                        : Image.asset(
                            imgPath,
                            fit: BoxFit.cover,
                          ),
                  );
                },
              ),
            ),

            // 2. ГРАДИЕНТ
            Positioned.fill(
              child: IgnorePointer(
                child: Container(
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    gradient: LinearGradient(
                      begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                      end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                      colors: [Colors.transparent, Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.8)],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),

            // 3. ТЕКСТОВЫЙ БЛОК
            Positioned(
              left: 12, right: 12, bottom: 12,
              child: IgnorePointer(
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    if (statusLabel.isNotEmpty) _buildStatusBadge(statusLabel),
                    Text(
                      title,
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        color: Colors.white, 
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, 
                        fontSize: 13, 
                        fontFamily: 'Orbitron',
                      ),
                      maxLines: 2,
                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),

            // 4. ЭЛЕМЕНТЫ УПРАВЛЕНИЯ КАРУСЕЛЬЮ
            if (_images.length > 1) ...[
              if (_currentPage > 0)
                Positioned(
                  left: 5, top: 0, bottom: 0,
                  child: Center(
                    child: _buildArrow(
                      Icons.arrow_back_ios_new, 
                      () => _pageController.previousPage(duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300), curve: Curves.ease),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              if (_currentPage < _images.length - 1)
                Positioned(
                  right: 5, top: 0, bottom: 0,
                  child: Center(
                    child: _buildArrow(
                      Icons.arrow_forward_ios, 
                      () => _pageController.nextPage(duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300), curve: Curves.ease),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              Positioned(
                bottom: 50, left: 0, right: 0,
                child: Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: List.generate(_images.length, (i) => _buildDot(i == _currentPage)),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _navigateToDetails() async {
    if (widget.onTap != null) {
      widget.onTap!();
      return;
    }

    final bloc = context.read<SearchBloc>();
    final int postId = _getPostId();
    final String category = _getCategory();

    if (postId == 0) return;

    bloc.add(LoadPostDetails(
      id: postId,
      category: category,
      locale: widget.currentLocale,
    ));

    final result = await Navigator.push(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(
        builder: (_) => BlocProvider.value(
          value: bloc, 
          child: PostCardPage(
            initialCategory: category,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );

    if (result == true && context.mounted) {
      bloc.add(LoadFilters(
        category: category, 
        locale: widget.currentLocale,
      )); 
    }
  }

  Widget _buildStatusBadge(String label) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 6),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: const Color(0xFF00F2FF).withValues(alpha: 0.1),
        border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFF00F2FF), width: 1),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
      ),
      child: Text(
        label, 
        style: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xFF00F2FF), fontSize: 9, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontFamily: 'Orbitron'),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildArrow(IconData icon, VoidCallback action) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: action,
      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
        decoration: const BoxDecoration(color: Colors.black26, shape: BoxShape.circle),
        child: Icon(icon, color: Colors.white, size: 16),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDot(bool active) {
    return AnimatedContainer(
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 2),
      height: 3, 
      width: active ? 10 : 3,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: active ? const Color(0xFF00F2FF) : Colors.white38, 
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
      ),
    );
  }
}-e 


=== FILE: lib/features/search/presentation/widgets/locale_selector.dart ===
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import '../bloc/search_bloc.dart';
import '../bloc/search_event.dart';

class LocaleSelector extends StatelessWidget {
  final String currentLocale;
  final bool isInNavBar; 

  const LocaleSelector({required this.currentLocale, this.isInNavBar = false, super.key});

  static const Map<String, List<String>> _groupedLocales = {
    "Europe": ["en", "uk", "pl", "nl", "be", "de", "fr", "it", "es", "pt", "cz", "sk", "ro"],
    "North America": ["ca", "us", "mx"],
  };

  static const Map<String, String> _fullNames = {
    "uk": "Ukraine (UA)", "en": "United Kingdom (EN)", "pl": "Poland (PL)",
    "nl": "Netherlands (NL)", "be": "Belgium", "de": "Germany (DE)",
    "fr": "France (FR)", "it": "Italy (IT)", "es": "Spain (ES)",
    "pt": "Portugal (PT)", "cz": "Czech Republic (CZ)", "sk": "Slovakia (SK)",
    "ro": "Romania (RO)", "ca": "Canada (CA)", "us": "USA (US)",
    "mx": "Mexico (MX)", "be_nl": "Flanders – NL", "be_fr": "Wallonia – FR",
    "be_de": "Eupen – DE"
  };

  String _getFlag(String code) {
    if (code.isEmpty) return "🌐";
    String countryCode = code.split('_')[0].toLowerCase();
    if (countryCode == 'en') countryCode = 'gb';
    if (countryCode == 'uk') countryCode = 'ua';
    return countryCode.toUpperCase().characters.map((char) => String.fromCharCode(char.codeUnitAt(0) + 127397)).join();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: () => _showLanguagePicker(context),
      child: Column( 
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Text(_getFlag(currentLocale), style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 22)),
          const SizedBox(height: 2),
          Text(
            currentLocale.toUpperCase(),
            style: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xFF1E293B), fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 10, fontFamily: 'Orbitron'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showLanguagePicker(BuildContext context) {
    final searchBloc = context.read<SearchBloc>();

    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF0A0E14),
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24))),
      builder: (_) {
        return BlocProvider.value(
          value: searchBloc,
          child: Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 20),
            child: ListView(
              shrinkWrap: true,
              children: _groupedLocales.entries.map((entry) {
                return ExpansionTile(
                  title: Text(entry.key, style: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xFF00F2FF), fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontFamily: 'Orbitron')),
                  children: entry.value.map((locale) {
                    // ✅ ЛОГИКА ВЕТВЛЕНИЯ ДЛЯ БЕЛЬГИИ
                    if (locale == 'be') {
                      return _buildBelgiumSubTile(context, searchBloc);
                    }
                    
                    return ListTile(
                      leading: Text(_getFlag(locale), style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 22)),
                      title: Text(_fullNames[locale] ?? locale.toUpperCase(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
                      onTap: () {
                        searchBloc.add(ChangeLocale(locale));
                        Navigator.pop(context);
                      },
                    );
                  }).toList(),
                );
              }).toList(),
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  // ========== БЕЛЬГИЙСКИЙ ПОДМОДУЛЬ ==========

  Widget _buildBelgiumSubTile(BuildContext context, SearchBloc bloc) {
    return ExpansionTile(
      leading: Text(_getFlag('be'), style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 20)),
      title: const Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.only(left: 16.0),
        child: Text("Belgium", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 18)),
      ),
      children: [
        _buildSubItem(context, bloc, 'be_nl'),
        _buildSubItem(context, bloc, 'be_fr'),
        _buildSubItem(context, bloc, 'be_de'),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSubItem(BuildContext context, SearchBloc bloc, String code) {
    return ListTile(
      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 48),
      leading: Text(_getFlag(code), style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 18)),
      title: Text(
        _fullNames[code] ?? code.toUpperCase(),
        style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white60, fontSize: 18),
      ),
      onTap: () {
        bloc.add(ChangeLocale(code));
        Navigator.pop(context);
      },
    );
  }
}-e

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../data/models/filter_model.dart';

class FilterBuilder extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<FilterModel> filters;
  final Map<String, dynamic> selectedValues;
  final String currentCategory;
  final Function(String filterId, dynamic value) onFilterChanged;

  const FilterBuilder({
    super.key,
    required this.filters,
    required this.selectedValues,
    required this.currentCategory,
    required this.onFilterChanged,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      key: ValueKey('list_$currentCategory'), // Принудительный ребилд при смене таба
      children: filters.map((filter) {
        final widgetKey = ValueKey('${currentCategory}_${filter.id}');

        return Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8.0, horizontal: 16.0),
          child: filter.type == 'dropdown' 
            ? _buildDropdown(filter, widgetKey)
            : _buildTextField(filter, widgetKey),
        );
      }).toList(),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDropdown(FilterModel filter, Key key) {
    final items = filter.options?.map((option) {
      return DropdownMenuItem<int>(value: option.id, child: Text(option.label));
    }).toList() ?? [];

    final currentValue = selectedValues[filter.id];
    final bool valueExists = items.any((item) => item.value == currentValue);

    return DropdownButtonFormField<int>(
      key: key,
      initialValue: valueExists ? (currentValue as int?) : null,
      decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: filter.label, border: const OutlineInputBorder()),
      items: items,
      onChanged: (val) => onFilterChanged(filter.id, val),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildTextField(FilterModel filter, Key key) {
    return TextFormField(
      key: key,
      initialValue: selectedValues[filter.id]?.toString(),
      decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: filter.label, border: const OutlineInputBorder()),
      onChanged: (val) => onFilterChanged(filter.id, val),
    );
  }
}-e

        trans['shared.map_picker.km'] ??

        trans['shared.map_picker.detect_my_location'] ??

        trans['shared.map_picker.no_search_center'] ??
        trans['filters.no_search_center'] ??
        'Центр не вибрано';

    final String geoStatusText = (searchLat != null && searchLng != null)
        ? '$centerPrefix: ${searchLat!.toStringAsFixed(3)}, ${searchLng!.toStringAsFixed(3)}'
        : noCenterText;

                const double defaultLng = 21.9991;
                onLocationSet(defaultLat, defaultLng);

                bloc.add(const UpdateFilterValue(filterId: 'search_lat', value: defaultLat));
                bloc.add(const UpdateFilterValue(filterId: 'search_lng', value: defaultLng));
                bloc.add(UpdateFilterValue(filterId: 'radius_km', value: radiusKm.round()));
                bloc.add(const PerformSearch());

              icon: const Icon(Icons.my_location, size: 16),
              label: Text(btnText.toUpperCase(), style: const TextStyle(fontFamily: 'Orbitron', fontSize: 10)),
              style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(

            style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 10, fontFamily: 'Monospace'),
