// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/search/presentation/widgets/filter_panel/thing_id_panel.dart
// LAYER: Presentation Layer (Widget)
// RESPONSIBILITY: Dynamic search panel for item metadata (VIN, IMEI, Serial, Documents)
//                 strictly utilizing YAML translation keys without hardcoded locales.
// =============================================================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/bloc/search_bloc.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/bloc/search_event.dart';

class ThingIdPanel extends StatelessWidget {
  final Map<String, TextEditingController> controllers;
  final Map<String, dynamic> trans;
  final String? subCategoryLabel;

  const ThingIdPanel({
    required this.controllers,
    required this.trans,
    required this.subCategoryLabel,
    super.key,
  });

  /// Универсальный поиск ключей перевода (поддерживает nested Map и dot-notation)
  static String? _lookup(Map<String, dynamic> trans, String dotPath) {
    if (trans.isEmpty) return null;

    // 1. Проверка плоского ключа ("thing_metadata.attributes.vin")
    if (trans.containsKey(dotPath) && trans[dotPath] != null && trans[dotPath].toString().isNotEmpty) {
      return trans[dotPath].toString();
    }

    // 2. Итеративный обход вложенного Map ("thing_metadata" -> "attributes" -> "vin")
    final parts = dotPath.split('.');
    dynamic current = trans;

    for (final part in parts) {
      if (current is Map) {
        dynamic foundKey;
        for (final k in current.keys) {
          if (k.toString().toLowerCase() == part.toLowerCase()) {
            foundKey = k;
            break;
          }
        }
        if (foundKey != null) {
          current = current[foundKey];
        } else {
          current = null;
          break;
        }
      } else {
        current = null;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (current != null && current is! Map && current.toString().isNotEmpty) {
      return current.toString();
    }

    return null;
  }

  /// Языконезависимое определение подкатегории (DE / EN / UK / PL)
  static List<String> getFieldsForCategoryLabel(String? label) {
    if (label == null || label.trim().isEmpty) return [];

    final String lower = label.toLowerCase().trim();

    // 1. DOCUMENTS / DOKUMENTE / DOKUMENTY / ДОКУМЕНТИ / ПАСПОРТ
    if (lower.contains('doc') || 
        lower.contains('dok') || 
        lower.contains('urk') || 
        lower.contains('ausweis') || 
        lower.contains('пасп') || 
        lower.contains('pasp') || 
        lower.contains('pass') || 
        lower.contains('посвідч') || 
        lower.contains('прав')) {
      return ['document_type', 'document_number', 'full_name'];
    } 
    // 2. PHONES / HANDY / ELEKTRONIK / TELEFONY / ТЕЛЕФОНИ
    else if (lower.contains('phon') || 
             lower.contains('handy') || 
             lower.contains('telef') || 
             lower.contains('gadg') || 
             lower.contains('ґадж') || 
             lower.contains('гадж') || 
             lower.contains('смартф') || 
             lower.contains('ноут') || 
             lower.contains('планш') ||
             lower.contains('elek') ||
             lower.contains('елек')) {
      return ['imei', 'serial_number', 'brand_model', 'color'];
    } 
    // 3. JEWELRY / SCHMUCK / BIŻUTERIA / ЮВЕЛІРНІ ВИРОБИ
    else if (lower.contains('jewel') || 
             lower.contains('schmuck') || 
             lower.contains('ювел') || 
             lower.contains('прикрас') || 
             lower.contains('biżut') || 
             lower.contains('bizut') ||
             lower.contains('кошт')) {
      return ['serial_number', 'brand_model', 'hallmark', 'engraving'];
    }
    // 4. CARS / FAHRZEUGE / AUTO / SAMOCHODY / АВТОМОБІЛІ & МОТОЦИКЛИ
    else if (lower.contains('car') || 
             lower.contains('fahrzeug') || 
             lower.contains('auto') || 
             lower.contains('pkw') || 
             lower.contains('kfz') || 
             lower.contains('авто') || 
             lower.contains('маш') || 
             lower.contains('samoch') || 
             lower.contains('moto') || 
             lower.contains('мото') || 
             lower.contains('трансп')) {
      return ['vin', 'license_plate', 'brand_model', 'color'];
    } 
    // 5. BICYCLES / FAHRRÄDER / ROWERY / ВЕЛОСИПЕДИ
    else if (lower.contains('bik') || 
             lower.contains('fahrrad') || 
             lower.contains('rad') || 
             lower.contains('вело') || 
             lower.contains('rower') || 
             lower.contains('самок')) {
      return ['frame_number', 'brand_model', 'color'];
    } 
    // 6. KEYS / SCHLÜSSEL / KLUCZE / КЛЮЧІ
    else if (lower.contains('key') || 
             lower.contains('schlüssel') || 
             lower.contains('schluessel') || 
             lower.contains('ключ') || 
             lower.contains('klucz')) {
      return ['key_type', 'keychain_description'];
    } 
    // 7. BAGS / TASCHEN / WALLETS / TORBY / СУМКИ / ГАМАНЦІ
    else if (lower.contains('bag') || 
             lower.contains('tasch') || 
             lower.contains('börs') || 
             lower.contains('boers') || 
             lower.contains('сумк') || 
             lower.contains('кошел') || 
             lower.contains('portf') || 
             lower.contains('wall') ||
             lower.contains('гаман') ||
             lower.contains('портф') ||
             lower.contains('рюкз')) {
      return ['brand_model', 'color', 'contents_description'];
    }

    // 8. DEFAULT FALLBACK
    return ['serial_number', 'brand_model', 'additional_info'];
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    const Color darkBg = Color(0xFF0F172A);
    const Color borderCyan = Color(0xFF00F2FF);

    final List<String> fields = getFieldsForCategoryLabel(subCategoryLabel);

    if (fields.isEmpty) {
      final String prompt = _lookup(trans, 'thing_metadata.select_category_prompt') ?? '';
      return Container(
        margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 12),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: darkBg,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          border: Border.all(color: Colors.white24),
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            const Icon(Icons.info_outline, color: borderCyan, size: 18),
            const SizedBox(width: 8),
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                prompt,
                style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }

    final String defaultTitle = _lookup(trans, 'thing_metadata.title') ?? 'IDENTIFIERS';
    final String titleText = subCategoryLabel != null
        ? '${defaultTitle.toUpperCase()}: ${subCategoryLabel!.toUpperCase()}'
        : defaultTitle;

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 12),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: darkBg,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: borderCyan.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(
            titleText.toUpperCase(),
            style: const TextStyle(
              color: borderCyan,
              fontFamily: 'Orbitron',
              fontSize: 10,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          ...fields.map((field) {
            final String label = _getAttrLabel(field, trans);
            final String placeholder = _getAttrPlaceholder(field, trans);
            final TextEditingController controller = controllers[field] ?? TextEditingController();

            return Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8.0),
              child: _idTextField(
                context,
                controller,
                placeholder.isNotEmpty ? placeholder : label,
                'q[thing_metadata_$field]',
                labelOverride: label,
              ),
            );
          }),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _idTextField(
    BuildContext context,
    TextEditingController controller,
    String hint,
    String filterKey, {
    String? labelOverride,
  }) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        if (labelOverride != null && labelOverride.isNotEmpty) ...[
          Text(
            labelOverride,
            style: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xFF00F2FF), fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
        ],
        TextField(
          controller: controller,
          style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 12),
          decoration: InputDecoration(
            hintText: hint,
            hintStyle: TextStyle(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.4), fontSize: 12),
            filled: true,
            fillColor: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.3),
            contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
            enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
              borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white24),
            ),
            focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
              borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Color(0xFF00F2FF)),
            ),
          ),
          onChanged: (val) {
            context.read<SearchBloc>().add(UpdateFilterValue(filterId: filterKey, value: val));
          },
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  String _getAttrLabel(String field, Map<String, dynamic> trans) {
    final paths = [
      'thing_metadata.attributes.$field',
      'thing_metadata_attributes_$field',
      'attributes.$field',
    ];
    for (final p in paths) {
      final res = _lookup(trans, p);
      if (res != null) return res;
    }
    return field.replaceAll('_', ' ').toUpperCase();
  }

  String _getAttrPlaceholder(String field, Map<String, dynamic> trans) {
    final paths = [
      'thing_metadata.placeholders.$field',
      'thing_metadata_placeholders_$field',
      'placeholders.$field',
    ];
    for (final p in paths) {
      final res = _lookup(trans, p);
      if (res != null) return res;
    }
    return '';
  }
}-e 


=== FILE: lib/features/search/presentation/widgets/filter_panel/filter_panel.dart ===
// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/search/presentation/widgets/filter_panel/filter_panel.dart
// LAYER: Presentation Layer (Widget)
// RESPONSIBILITY: Main filter dashboard combining category tabs, FilterBuilder,
//                 and expandable sub-panels for ID specs and geolocation.
// =============================================================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';

import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/bloc/search_bloc.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/bloc/search_event.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/bloc/search_state.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/widgets/filter_builder.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/widgets/filter_panel/animal_id_panel.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/widgets/filter_panel/thing_id_panel.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/widgets/filter_panel/geo_radius_panel.dart';

class FilterPanel extends StatefulWidget {
  final SearchState state;
  final Map<String, dynamic> translations;
  final String currentLocale;
  final dynamic filters;
  final Map<String, dynamic> selectedFilterValues;
  final String activeCategory;
  final Function(String cat) onCategoryChanged;

  final TextEditingController chipController;
  final TextEditingController passportController;
  final TextEditingController insuranceController;
  final Map<String, TextEditingController> thingControllers;

  final double radiusKm;
  final double? searchLat;
  final double? searchLng;
  final Function(double rad) onRadiusChanged;
  final Function(double lat, double lng) onLocationSet;
  final VoidCallback onResetGeoAndControllers;

  const FilterPanel({
    required this.state,
    required this.translations,
    required this.currentLocale,
    required this.filters,
    required this.selectedFilterValues,
    required this.activeCategory,
    required this.onCategoryChanged,
    required this.chipController,
    required this.passportController,
    required this.insuranceController,
    required this.thingControllers,
    required this.radiusKm,
    required this.searchLat,
    required this.searchLng,
    required this.onRadiusChanged,
    required this.onLocationSet,
    required this.onResetGeoAndControllers,
    super.key,
  });

  @override
  State<FilterPanel> createState() => _FilterPanelState();
}

class _FilterPanelState extends State<FilterPanel> {
  bool _isAdvancedIdOpen = false;
  bool _isGeoPanelOpen = false;

  String? _getSelectedSubCategoryLabel(dynamic filters, Map<String, dynamic> selectedValues) {
    if (filters == null) return null;

    dynamic selectedId;
    selectedValues.forEach((k, v) {
      if ((k.contains('phone_id') || k.contains('cat_id') || k.contains('sub_category')) && v != null) {
        selectedId = v;
      }
    });

    if (selectedId == null) return null;

    if (filters is List) {
      for (final f in filters) {
        if (f.options != null) {
          for (final opt in f.options) {
            if (opt.id.toString() == selectedId.toString()) {
              return opt.label;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  int _getFieldsCountForCategory(String? label) {
    return ThingIdPanel.getFieldsForCategoryLabel(label).length;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    const Color darkSlate = Color(0xFF1E293B);
    const Color neonBlue = Color(0xFF00F2FF);
    final bool isAnimalCategory = widget.activeCategory == 'animals';
    final bool isThingCategory = widget.activeCategory == 'things';
    final bool hasIdPanel = isAnimalCategory || isThingCategory;

    final String? selectedSubCategoryLabel = _getSelectedSubCategoryLabel(
      widget.filters,
      widget.selectedFilterValues,
    );
    final int fieldsCount = _getFieldsCountForCategory(selectedSubCategoryLabel);

    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 10, 16, 20),
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: darkSlate,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
          border: Border.all(color: darkSlate, width: 2),
        ),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            _buildCategoryButtons(context, widget.activeCategory, widget.translations, widget.currentLocale),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            _buildSearchField(context, widget.translations),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            if (widget.filters != null) ...[
              Theme(
                data: Theme.of(context).copyWith(
                  canvasColor: Colors.white,
                  inputDecorationTheme: InputDecorationTheme(
                    filled: true,
                    fillColor: Colors.white,
                    contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 16, right: 16, top: 24, bottom: 10),
                    floatingLabelStyle: const TextStyle(color: neonBlue, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16),
                    enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                      borderSide: const BorderSide(color: darkSlate),
                    ),
                    focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                      borderSide: const BorderSide(color: neonBlue, width: 2),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                child: FilterBuilder(
                  filters: widget.filters,
                  selectedValues: widget.selectedFilterValues,
                  currentCategory: widget.activeCategory,
                  onFilterChanged: (id, val) =>
                      context.read<SearchBloc>().add(UpdateFilterValue(filterId: id, value: val)),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
            ],
            Row(
              children: [
                if (hasIdPanel) ...[
                  Expanded(
                    child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                      onPressed: () => setState(() => _isAdvancedIdOpen = !_isAdvancedIdOpen),
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.security, size: 16),
                      label: Text(
                        fieldsCount > 0 ? 'ID ($fieldsCount)' : 'ID',
                        style: const TextStyle(fontFamily: 'Orbitron', fontSize: 11),
                      ),
                      style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                        foregroundColor: const Color(0xFF00F2FF),
                        side: const BorderSide(color: Color(0xFF00F2FF)),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                ],
                Expanded(
                  child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                    onPressed: () => setState(() => _isGeoPanelOpen = !_isGeoPanelOpen),
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.my_location, size: 16),
                    label: const Text('Geo', style: TextStyle(fontFamily: 'Orbitron', fontSize: 11)),
                    style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                      foregroundColor: const Color(0xFFFF8A00),
                      side: const BorderSide(color: Color(0xFFFF8A00)),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            if (isAnimalCategory && _isAdvancedIdOpen)
              AnimalIdPanel(
                chipController: widget.chipController,
                passportController: widget.passportController,
                insuranceController: widget.insuranceController,
                trans: widget.translations,
              ),
            if (isThingCategory && _isAdvancedIdOpen)
              ThingIdPanel(
                controllers: widget.thingControllers,
                trans: widget.translations,
                subCategoryLabel: selectedSubCategoryLabel,
              ),
            if (_isGeoPanelOpen)
              GeoRadiusPanel(
                radiusKm: widget.radiusKm,
                searchLat: widget.searchLat,
                searchLng: widget.searchLng,
                trans: widget.translations,
                onRadiusChanged: widget.onRadiusChanged,
                onLocationSet: widget.onLocationSet,
              ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            if (widget.filters != null)
              _buildApplyFiltersButton(context, widget.translations),
            if (widget.state is SearchSuccess)
              _buildResetFiltersButton(context, widget.currentLocale, widget.activeCategory, widget.translations),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSearchField(BuildContext context, Map<String, dynamic> translations) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.white, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
      child: TextField(
        style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.black),
        decoration: InputDecoration(
          hintText: translations['plac_title'] ?? 'Пошук...',
          prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search, color: Colors.grey),
          suffixIcon: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(6.0),
            child: ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: () => context.read<SearchBloc>().add(const PerformSearch()),
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFFF8A00)),
              child: Text(translations['find'] ?? 'FIND', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 12)),
            ),
          ),
          border: InputBorder.none,
          contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 15, horizontal: 15),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCategoryButtons(
    BuildContext context,
    String activeCategory,
    Map<String, dynamic> translations,
    String currentLocale,
  ) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        _categoryTab(context, 'people', translations['button_article'] ?? 'Люди', const Color(0xFF00F2FF), activeCategory == 'people', currentLocale),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        _categoryTab(context, 'animals', translations['button_sense'] ?? 'Тварини', const Color(0xFFFF8A00), activeCategory == 'animals', currentLocale),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        _categoryTab(context, 'things', translations['button_thing'] ?? 'Речі', const Color(0xFF2ECC71), activeCategory == 'things', currentLocale),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _categoryTab(
    BuildContext context,
    String cat,
    String label,
    Color color,
    bool isActive,
    String locale,
  ) {
    return Expanded(
      child: GestureDetector(
        onTap: () {
          setState(() {
            _isAdvancedIdOpen = false;
          });
          widget.onCategoryChanged(cat);
          context.read<SearchBloc>().add(LoadFilters(category: cat, locale: locale));
        },
        child: Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: isActive ? color : Colors.white,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFF1E293B), width: 2),
          ),
          child: Center(
            child: Text(
              label,
              style: TextStyle(
                color: isActive ? Colors.white : const Color(0xFF1E293B),
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                fontSize: 13,
                fontFamily: 'Orbitron',
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildApplyFiltersButton(BuildContext context, Map<String, dynamic> translations) {
    return SizedBox(
      width: double.infinity,
      child: ElevatedButton.icon(
        onPressed: () => context.read<SearchBloc>().add(const PerformSearch()),
        icon: const Icon(Icons.filter_list),
        label: Text(translations['filter_button'] ?? "Застосувати фильтри"),
        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
          backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFFF8A00).withValues(alpha: 0.1),
          foregroundColor: const Color(0xFFFF8A00),
          side: const BorderSide(color: Color(0xFFFF8A00)),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildResetFiltersButton(
    BuildContext context,
    String currentLocale,
    String activeCategory,
    Map<String, dynamic> translations,
  ) {
    return TextButton.icon(
      onPressed: () {
        setState(() {
          _isAdvancedIdOpen = false;
          _isGeoPanelOpen = false;
        });
        widget.onResetGeoAndControllers();
        context.read<SearchBloc>().add(LoadFilters(category: activeCategory, locale: currentLocale));
      },
      icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh, size: 16),
      label: Text(translations['last'] ?? translations['reset_filters'] ?? "Скинути фильтри"),
      style: TextButton.styleFrom(foregroundColor: const Color(0xFF00F2FF)),
    );
  }

// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/search/presentation/widgets/search_results_grid.dart
// LAYER: Presentation Layer (Widget)
// RESPONSIBILITY: Responsive 2-column SliverGrid rendering ArticleCard items
//                 and PaginationBar footer inside a SliverMainAxisGroup.
// =============================================================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/widgets/articles_card.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/widgets/pagination_bar.dart';

class SearchResultsGrid extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<dynamic> results;
  final String currentLocale;
  final int currentPage;
  final int lastPage;
  final int totalCount;

  const SearchResultsGrid({
    required this.results,
    required this.currentLocale,
    this.currentPage = 1,
    this.lastPage = 1,
    this.totalCount = 0,
    super.key,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SliverMainAxisGroup(
      slivers: [
        SliverPadding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
          sliver: SliverGrid(
            gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(
              crossAxisCount: 2,
              childAspectRatio: 0.68,
              crossAxisSpacing: 10,
              mainAxisSpacing: 10,
            ),
            delegate: SliverChildBuilderDelegate(
              (context, index) {
                return ArticleCard(
                  post: results[index],
                  currentLocale: currentLocale,
                );
              },
              childCount: results.length,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        SliverToBoxAdapter(
          child: PaginationBar(
            currentPage: currentPage,
            lastPage: lastPage,
            totalCount: totalCount,
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

// LAYER: Presentation Layer (Widget)

// =============================================================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/widgets/locale_selector.dart';

class CustomBottomNavBar extends StatelessWidget {
  final int currentIndex;
  final String currentLocale;
  final Function(int) onTap;
  final Map<String, dynamic> translations;
  final int unreadCount;

  const CustomBottomNavBar({
    required this.currentIndex,
    required this.currentLocale,
    required this.onTap,
    required this.translations,
    this.unreadCount = 0,
    super.key,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final double bottomInset = MediaQuery.of(context).padding.bottom;

    return Padding(
      padding: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: bottomInset + 12),
      child: Container(
        margin: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(40, 0, 40, 0),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4, horizontal: 12),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: Colors.white,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(30),
          border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFF1E293B), width: 2),
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
              color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
              blurRadius: 20,
            ),
          ],
        ),
        child: Row(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround,
          children: [
            LocaleSelector(
              currentLocale: currentLocale,
              isInNavBar: true,
            ),
            _navItem(1, Icons.search, translations['nav_search'] ?? 'Search'),
            const SizedBox(width: 40),
            _navItem(
              3,
              Icons.mail_outline,
              translations['nav_messages'] ?? 'Chat',
              badgeCount: unreadCount,
            ),
            _navItem(4, Icons.person_outline, translations['nav_profile'] ?? 'Profile'),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _navItem(
    int index,
    IconData icon,
    String label, {
    int badgeCount = 0,
  }) {
    final bool isActive = currentIndex == index;
    const Color activeColor = Color(0xFFD81B60);
    const Color inactiveColor = Color(0xFF1E293B);

    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: () => onTap(index),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Badge(
            label: Text(
              '$badgeCount',
              style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 10),
            ),
            isLabelVisible: badgeCount > 0,
            backgroundColor: Colors.red,
            child: Icon(
              icon,
              color: isActive ? activeColor : inactiveColor,
              size: 24,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 2),
          Text(
            label,
            style: TextStyle(
              color: isActive ? activeColor : inactiveColor,
              fontSize: 10,
              fontFamily: 'Orbitron',
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

=== FILE: lib/features/search/presentation/widgets/pagination_bar.dart ===
//import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';

import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/bloc/search_bloc.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/bloc/search_event.dart';

class PaginationBar extends StatelessWidget {
  final int currentPage;
  final int lastPage;
  final int totalCount;

  const PaginationBar({
    required this.currentPage,
    required this.lastPage,
    required this.totalCount,
    super.key,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    const Color darkSlate = Color(0xFF1E293B);
    const Color neonOrange = Color(0xFFFF8A00);
    const Color neonBlue = Color(0xFF00F2FF);

    final bool canGoPrevious = currentPage > 1;
    final bool canGoNext = currentPage < lastPage;

    debugPrint(
      '[PAGINATION] Widget build: '
      'currentPage=$currentPage, '
      'lastPage=$lastPage, '
      'totalCount=$totalCount, '
      'canGoPrevious=$canGoPrevious, '
      'canGoNext=$canGoNext',
    );

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
        horizontal: 16,
        vertical: 16,
      ),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
        horizontal: 16,
        vertical: 12,
      ),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: darkSlate,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.white12),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          // Previous page
          IconButton(
            onPressed: canGoPrevious
                ? () {
                    debugPrint(
                      '[PAGINATION] Previous tapped: '
                      'currentPage=$currentPage, '
                      'lastPage=$lastPage',
                    );

                    final int targetPage = currentPage - 1;

                    debugPrint(
                      '[PAGINATION] Dispatching '
                      'ChangePage($targetPage)',
                    );

                    context.read<SearchBloc>().add(
                      ChangePage(targetPage),
                    );
                  }
                : null,
            icon: const Icon(
              Icons.arrow_back_ios_new,
              size: 18,
            ),
            color: canGoPrevious ? neonBlue : Colors.white24,
          ),

          // Page counter
          Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              Text(
                '$currentPage / $lastPage',
                style: const TextStyle(
                  color: Colors.white,
                  fontFamily: 'Orbitron',
                  fontSize: 14,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 2),
              Text(
                'Total: $totalCount',
                style: const TextStyle(
                  color: Colors.white54,
                  fontSize: 10,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),

          // Next page
          IconButton(
            onPressed: canGoNext
                ? () {
                    debugPrint(
                      '[PAGINATION] Next tapped: '
                      'currentPage=$currentPage, '
                      'lastPage=$lastPage, '
                      'totalCount=$totalCount',
                    );

                    final int targetPage = currentPage + 1;

                    debugPrint(
                      '[PAGINATION] Dispatching '
                      'ChangePage($targetPage)',
                    );

                    context.read<SearchBloc>().add(
                      ChangePage(targetPage),
                    );
                  }
                : null,
            icon: const Icon(
              Icons.arrow_forward_ios,
              size: 18,
            ),
            color: canGoNext ? neonOrange : Colors.white24,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/search/presentation/widgets/post_button_panel.dart
// LAYER: Presentation Layer (Widget)
// RESPONSIBILITY: Renders neon action control panel (Back, Chat, Comments, Edit, Delete)
//                 with glow effects, haptic feedback, and responsive layout.
// =============================================================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';

class PostButtonPanel extends StatelessWidget {
  final Map<String, dynamic> tr;
  final VoidCallback onMessageTap;
  final VoidCallback onCommentTap;
  final VoidCallback onEditTap;
  final VoidCallback onDeleteTap;
  final bool showEdit;
  final bool showDelete;

  const PostButtonPanel({
    super.key,
    required this.tr,
    required this.onMessageTap,
    required this.onCommentTap,
    required this.onEditTap,
    required this.onDeleteTap,
    this.showEdit = false,
    this.showDelete = false,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Center(
      child: SingleChildScrollView(
        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
        physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8.0, horizontal: 4.0),
          child: Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              // 1. НАЗАД
              _actionButton(
                icon: Icons.arrow_back_rounded,
                color: const Color(0xFF94A3B8),
                accentColor: const Color(0xFF00F2FF),
                onTap: () => Navigator.pop(context),
                tooltip: tr['back'] ?? 'Назад',
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),

              // 1. ЧАТ / ЛС (Самолётик под углом 45°)
              _actionButton(
                icon: Icons.near_me_rounded,
                color: const Color(0xFF00F2FF),
                accentColor: const Color(0xFF00F2FF),
                onTap: onMessageTap,
                tooltip: tr['message'] ?? 'Написати автору',
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
             
              // 2. КОММЕНТАРИИ (Форум / Публичный тред)
              _actionButton(
                icon: Icons.forum_rounded,
                color: const Color(0xFFFF8A00),
                accentColor: const Color(0xFFFF8A00),
                onTap: onCommentTap,
                tooltip: tr['comment'] ?? 'Коментарі',
              ),

              // 3. РЕДАКТИРОВАНИЕ (Блокнот с карандашом)
              if (showEdit) ...[
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                _actionButton(
                  icon: Icons.edit_outlined,
                  color: const Color(0xFF3B82F6),
                  accentColor: const Color(0xFF3B82F6),
                  onTap: onEditTap,
                  tooltip: tr['edit'] ?? 'Редагувати',
                ),
              ],

              // 4. УДАЛЕНИЕ (опционально)
              if (showDelete) ...[
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                _actionButton(
                  icon: Icons.delete_outline_rounded,
                  color: const Color(0xFFEF4444),
                  accentColor: const Color(0xFFEF4444),
                  onTap: onDeleteTap,
                  tooltip: tr['delete'] ?? 'Видалити',
                ),
              ],
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _actionButton({
    required IconData icon,
    required Color color,
    required Color accentColor,
    required VoidCallback onTap,
    String? tooltip,
  }) {
    return Tooltip(
      message: tooltip ?? '',
      child: Material(
        color: Colors.transparent,
        child: InkWell(
          onTap: () {
            HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
            onTap();
          },
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
          splashColor: accentColor.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
          highlightColor: accentColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
          child: Container(
            width: 52,
            height: 52,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: const Color(0xFF0F172A),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
              border: Border.all(
                color: accentColor.withValues(alpha: 0.4),
                width: 1.5,
              ),
              boxShadow: [
                BoxShadow(
                  color: accentColor.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                  blurRadius: 10,
                  spreadRadius: 1,
                  offset: const Offset(0, 2),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            child: Icon(
              icon,
              color: color,
              size: 22,
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/search/presentation/widgets/title_layout/category_card.dart
// LAYER: Presentation Layer (Widget)
// RESPONSIBILITY: Renders single title category banner card with background images.
// =============================================================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/domain/models/findway_category.dart';

class CategoryCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final FindWayCategory category;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const CategoryCard({
    required this.category,
    required this.onTap,
    super.key,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final String path = category.imagePath;
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),
      height: 250,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
        image: DecorationImage(
          image: path.startsWith('http')
              ? NetworkImage(path) as ImageProvider
              : AssetImage(path) as ImageProvider,
          fit: BoxFit.cover,
          colorFilter: ColorFilter.mode(Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.55), BlendMode.darken),
        ),
      ),
      child: ClipRRect(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
        child: Material(
          color: Colors.transparent,
          child: InkWell(
            onTap: onTap,
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20.0),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,
                children: [
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Container(
                        width: 7,
                        height: 7,
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(color: category.accentColor, shape: BoxShape.circle),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Text(
                        category.modelsInfo.toUpperCase(),
                        style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 9, fontFamily: 'Orbitron'),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Text(
                    category.title.toUpperCase(),
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: category.accentColor,
                      fontSize: 28,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                      fontFamily: 'Orbitron',
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Text(
                    category.description,
                    style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 13),
                    maxLines: 3,
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  ),
                ],

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';

import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/domain/models/findway_category.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/bloc/search_bloc.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/bloc/search_event.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/widgets/locale_selector.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/widgets/title_layout/category_card.dart';

class TitleLayout extends StatelessWidget {
  final ScrollController scrollController;
  final Map<String, dynamic> translations;
  final String currentLocale;
  final Function(String categoryKey) onCategorySelected;

  const TitleLayout({
    required this.scrollController,
    required this.translations,
    required this.currentLocale,
    required this.onCategorySelected,
    super.key,
  });

  List<FindWayCategory> _getLocalizedCategories(Map<String, dynamic> trans) {
    return [
      FindWayCategory(
        title: trans['things'] ?? trans['title_three'] ?? 'Things',
        accentColor: const Color(0xFFFF8A00),
        modelsInfo: 'Models EfficientNet-B0, NLP | ACTIVE',
        description: trans['things_desc'] ?? trans['description_three'] ?? '',
        imagePath: 'assets/images/things.png',
      ),
      FindWayCategory(
        title: trans['animals'] ?? trans['title_two'] ?? 'Animals',
        accentColor: const Color(0xFF00F2FF),
        modelsInfo: 'Models EfficientNet-B0, NLP & YOLO8/DINOv2 | ACTIVE',
        description: trans['animals_desc'] ?? trans['description_two'] ?? '',
        imagePath: 'assets/images/cat.png',
      ),
      FindWayCategory(
        title: trans['people'] ?? trans['title_one'] ?? 'People',
        accentColor: const Color(0xFF3B82F6),
        modelsInfo: 'Models EfficientNet-B0, NLP | ACTIVE & ArcFace INACTIVE',
        description: trans['people_desc'] ?? trans['description_one'] ?? '',
        imagePath: 'assets/images/peop.png',
      ),
    ];
  }

  String _getRawCategoryName(int index) {
    switch (index) {
      case 0:
        return 'things';
      case 1:
        return 'animals';
      case 2:
        return 'people';
      default:
        return 'things';
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final categories = _getLocalizedCategories(translations);

    return CustomScrollView(
      controller: scrollController,
      physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
      slivers: [
        SliverAppBar(
          backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF0F4F8),
          elevation: 0,
          floating: true,
          title: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 5.0),
            child: GestureDetector(
              onTap: () {
                HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
                onCategorySelected('');
                final bloc = context.read<SearchBloc>();
                bloc.add(LoadFilters(category: '', locale: bloc.currentLocale));
              },
              child: Image.asset(
                'assets/images/logo1.png',
                height: 65,
                fit: BoxFit.contain,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 16.0),
              child: LocaleSelector(currentLocale: currentLocale),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        const SliverToBoxAdapter(child: SizedBox(height: 12)),
        SliverPadding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
          sliver: SliverList(
            delegate: SliverChildBuilderDelegate(
              (context, index) {
                final String categoryKey = _getRawCategoryName(index);
                return CategoryCard(
                  category: categories[index],
                  onTap: () {
                    HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
                    onCategorySelected(categoryKey);
                    context.read<SearchBloc>().add(
                          LoadFilters(category: categoryKey, locale: currentLocale),
                        );
                  },
                );
              },
              childCount: categories.length,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        SliverToBoxAdapter( //
          child: SizedBox(
            height: 10.0 + MediaQuery.of(context).padding.bottom,
          ),
        ),
      ],

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';

import '../bloc/search_bloc.dart';
import '../bloc/search_event.dart';
import '../bloc/search_state.dart';
import '../../../../features/comments/presentation/bloc/comments_bloc.dart';
import '../../../../features/comments/presentation/bloc/comments_event.dart';
import '../../../../injection_container.dart';
import '../widgets/post_button_panel.dart';
import '../widgets/map/show_map_widget.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/comments/presentation/widgets/comments_section.dart';

class PostCardPage extends StatelessWidget {
  final String? initialCategory;

  const PostCardPage({
    this.initialCategory,
    super.key,
  });

  void _onEditTapped(BuildContext context, dynamic post, SearchBloc searchBloc) async {
    final String category = _getSafeString(post, 'category', fallback: initialCategory ?? 'people');
    final int postId = _getSafeInt(post, 'id');

    await Navigator.pushNamed(context, '/post_edit', arguments: {
      'bloc': searchBloc,
      'postId': postId,
      'initialCategory': category,
      'initialTitle': _getSafeString(post, 'title'),
      'initialText': _getSafeString(post, 'text'),
      'initialLocalId': null,
      'initialChoiceId': null,
      'initialActionId': null,
      'existingImages': _getSafeList(post, 'images'),
    });

    if (context.mounted) {
      searchBloc.add(LoadPostDetails(
        id: postId,
        category: category,
        locale: searchBloc.currentLocale,
      ));
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: Colors.white,
      resizeToAvoidBottomInset: true,
      body: BlocListener<SearchBloc, SearchState>(
        listener: (context, state) {
          if (state is PostDeleteSuccess) {
            final msg = state.uiTranslations['post_destroy_success'] ?? "Deleted successfully!";
            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
              SnackBar(
                content: Text(msg),
                backgroundColor: Colors.orange,
              ),
            );
            Navigator.pop(context, true);
          }

          if (state is SearchError) {
            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
              SnackBar(
                content: Text(state.message),
                backgroundColor: Colors.redAccent,
              ),
            );
          }
        },
        child: BlocBuilder<SearchBloc, SearchState>(
          builder: (context, state) {
            if (state is PostDetailsLoading) {
              return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
            }

            if (state is PostDetailsLoaded) {
              final post = state.post;
              final tr = state.uiTranslations;
              final int postId = _getSafeInt(post, 'id');
              final String category = _getSafeString(post, 'category', fallback: initialCategory ?? 'people');
              final double? lat = _getSafeDouble(post, 'lat');
              final double? lng = _getSafeDouble(post, 'lng');
              final String address = _getSafeString(post, 'address');
              final String title = _getSafeString(post, 'title');

              return BlocProvider(
                create: (context) => sl<CommentsBloc>(
                  param1: postId,
                  param2: _mapCategory(category),
                )..add(const FetchComments()),
                child: Builder(
                  builder: (pageContext) {
                    return CustomScrollView(
                      slivers: [
                        _buildGallery(post),
                        SliverToBoxAdapter(
                          child: Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                            child: Column(
                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                              children: [
                                _buildAuthorHeader(post),
                                const Divider(height: 32),
                                Text(
                                  title,
                                  style: const TextStyle(
                                    fontSize: 24,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  ),
                                ),
                                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                                Text(
                                  _getSafeString(post, 'text'),
                                  style: const TextStyle(
                                    fontSize: 16,
                                    height: 1.6,
                                    color: Colors.black87,
                                  ),
                                ),
                                const SizedBox(height: 16),

                                // Метаданные животных.
                                _buildAnimalMetadataCard(post, tr),

                                // Метаданные вещей.
                                _buildThingMetadataCard(post, tr),

                                _buildLocationRow(post),
                                const SizedBox(height: 16),

                                // Интерактивная карта OpenStreetMap / Leaflet.
                                if (lat != null && lng != null)
                                  ShowMapWidget(
                                    lat: lat,
                                    lng: lng,
                                    title: title,
                                    address: address,
                                    translations: tr,
                                  ),

                                const SizedBox(height: 24),
                                PostButtonPanel(
                                  tr: tr,
                                  showEdit: _getCanEdit(post),
                                  showDelete: _getCanDelete(post),
                                  onCommentTap: () => CommentsSection.showAddCommentSheet(pageContext, tr),
                                  onEditTap: () => _onEditTapped(context, post, context.read<SearchBloc>()),
                                  onDeleteTap: () => _showDeleteConfirmation(context, postId, category, tr),
                                  onMessageTap: () {
                                    final searchBloc = context.read<SearchBloc>();
                                    final authorId = _getAuthorId(post);
                                    final authorName = _getAuthorName(post);
                                    final avatarUrl = _getAuthorAvatar(post);

                                    Navigator.pushNamed(context, '/chat', arguments: {
                                      'recipientId': authorId,
                                      'username': authorName,
                                      'avatarUrl': avatarUrl,
                                      'currentLocale': searchBloc.currentLocale,
                                    });
                                  },
                                ),
                                const SizedBox(height: 32),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                        SliverToBoxAdapter(
                          child: Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16.0),
                            child: CommentsSection(tr: tr),
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SliverToBoxAdapter(child: SizedBox(height: 100)),
                      ],
                    );
                  },
                ),
              );
            }

            return const SizedBox.shrink();
          },
        ),
      ),
    );

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Метаданные животных.
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Widget _buildAnimalMetadataCard(dynamic post, Map<String, dynamic> tr) {
    final meta = _getAnimalMeta(post);

    if (meta.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();

    return _buildMetadataCard(
      title: tr['animal_identifiers'] ??
          tr['senses.show.animal_identifiers'] ??
          'ІДЕНТИФІКАТОРИ ТВАРИНИ',
      metadata: meta,
      icon: Icons.pets_outlined,
    );
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Метаданные вещей.
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Widget _buildThingMetadataCard(dynamic post, Map<String, dynamic> tr) {
    final meta = _getThingMeta(post);

    if (meta.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();

    return _buildMetadataCard(
      title: tr['thing_details'] ??
          tr['things.show.details'] ??
          'ХАРАКТЕРИСТИКИ ВЕЩИ',
      metadata: meta,
      icon: Icons.inventory_2_outlined,
    );
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Универсальная карточка метаданных.
  // Отображает все заполненные поля из animal_metadata и thing_metadata.
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Widget _buildMetadataCard({
    required String title,
    required Map<String, dynamic> metadata,
    required IconData icon,
  }) {
    final entries = metadata.entries.where((entry) {
      final value = entry.value;

      if (value == null) return false;
      if (value is String && value.trim().isEmpty) return false;
      if (value is Map && value.isEmpty) return false;
      if (value is List && value.isEmpty) return false;

      return true;
    }).toList();

    if (entries.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();

    String formatLabel(String key) {
      return key
          .replaceAll('_', ' ')
          .split(' ')
          .where((part) => part.isNotEmpty)
          .map((part) => '${part[0].toUpperCase()}${part.substring(1)}')
          .join(' ');
    }

    String formatValue(dynamic value) {
      if (value is Map) {
        return value.entries
            .map((entry) =>
                '${formatLabel(entry.key.toString())}: ${formatValue(entry.value)}')
            .join(', ');
      }

      if (value is List) {
        return value.map(formatValue).join(', ');
      }

      if (value is bool) {
        return value ? 'Yes' : 'No';
      }

      return value.toString();
    }

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 20),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: const Color(0xFF0F172A),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(
          color: const Color(0xFF00F2FF).withValues(alpha: 0.3),
        ),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Icon(icon, color: const Color(0xFF00F2FF), size: 16),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  title.toUpperCase(),
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    color: Color(0xFF00F2FF),
                    fontFamily: 'Orbitron',
                    fontSize: 11,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const Divider(color: Colors.white12, height: 20),
          ...entries.map(
            (entry) => Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
              child: Row(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Expanded(
                    flex: 2,
                    child: Text(
                      '${formatLabel(entry.key)}:',
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        color: Colors.white60,
                        fontSize: 12,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(
                    flex: 3,
                    child: Text(
                      formatValue(entry.value),
                      textAlign: TextAlign.end,
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        color: Color(0xFF00F2FF),
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        fontSize: 12,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _specRow(String label, String value, Color valueColor) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4.0),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Text(
            label,
            style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white60, fontSize: 12),
          ),
          Text(
            value,
            style: TextStyle(
              color: valueColor,
              fontFamily: 'Monospace',
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              fontSize: 12,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  String _mapCategory(String? rawType) {
    final type = rawType?.toLowerCase() ?? 'article';

    if (type == 'article' || type == 'people') return 'people';
    if (type == 'sense' || type == 'animal' || type == 'animals') return 'animals';
    if (type == 'thing' || type == 'things') return 'things';

    return 'people';
  }

  Widget _buildGallery(dynamic post) {
    final List<String> images = _getSafeList(post, 'images');

    return SliverAppBar(
      expandedHeight: 350.0,
      pinned: true,
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF0A0E14),
      flexibleSpace: FlexibleSpaceBar(
        background: _PostDetailsGallery(images: images),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildAuthorHeader(dynamic post) {
    final String username = _getAuthorName(post);
    final String? avatarUrl = _getAuthorAvatar(post);
    final String createdAt = _getSafeString(
      post,
      'created_at',
      fallback: _getSafeString(post, 'createdAt'),
    );

    return Row(
      children: [
        CircleAvatar(
          backgroundImage: avatarUrl != null ? NetworkImage(avatarUrl) : null,
          child: avatarUrl == null ? const Icon(Icons.person) : null,
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 12),
        Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            const Text(
              "POSTED BY",
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10, color: Colors.grey),
            ),
            Text(
              username,
              style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        const Spacer(),
        Text(
          createdAt,
          style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.grey, fontSize: 12),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLocationRow(dynamic post) {
    final String localTitle = _getSafeString(post, 'local');

    if (localTitle.isEmpty) {
      return const SizedBox.shrink();
    }

    return Row(
      children: [
        const Icon(Icons.location_on, color: Colors.blueAccent, size: 18),
        const SizedBox(width: 4),
        Text(
          localTitle,
          style: const TextStyle(
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            color: Colors.blueAccent,
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  void _showDeleteConfirmation(
    BuildContext context,
    int postId,
    String category,
    Map<String, dynamic> trData,
  ) {
    final searchBloc = context.read<SearchBloc>();

    String t(String key, String fallback) {
      final fullKey = 'delete.$key';
      return (trData[fullKey] ?? trData[key] ?? fallback).toString();
    }

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (confirmContext) => AlertDialog(
        backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF1E293B),
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        ),
        title: Text(
          t('delete_title', 'Delete?'),
          style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
        ),
        content: Text(
          t('delete_message', 'Are you sure?'),
          style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(confirmContext),
            child: Text(
              t('cancel', 'CANCEL'),
              style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white60),
            ),
          ),
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () {
              searchBloc.add(DeletePost(postId: postId, category: category));
              Navigator.pop(confirmContext);
            },
            child: Text(
              t('confirm', 'DELETE'),
              style: const TextStyle(
                color: Colors.redAccent,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Безопасные универсальные считыватели полей.
  // Поддерживают Map и объекты моделей.
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

  String _getSafeString(
    dynamic obj,
    String key, {
    String fallback = '',
  }) {
    if (obj == null) return fallback;

    try {
      if (obj is Map) return obj[key]?.toString() ?? fallback;

      final val = (obj as dynamic).toJson()[key] ?? (obj as dynamic).toString();
      return val.toString();
    } catch (_) {
      try {
        switch (key) {
          case 'title':
            return obj.title?.toString() ?? fallback;
          case 'text':
            return obj.text?.toString() ?? fallback;
          case 'category':
            return obj.category?.toString() ?? fallback;
          case 'local':
            return obj.local?.toString() ?? fallback;
          case 'address':
            return obj.address?.toString() ?? fallback;
          case 'created_at':
          case 'createdAt':
            return obj.createdAt?.toString() ??
                obj.created_at?.toString() ??
                fallback;
        }
      } catch (_) {}
    }

    return fallback;
  }

  int _getSafeInt(dynamic obj, String key) {
    if (obj == null) return 0;

    try {
      if (obj is Map) return int.tryParse(obj[key].toString()) ?? 0;

      return (obj.id as int?) ?? 0;
    } catch (_) {
      return 0;
    }
  }

  double? _getSafeDouble(dynamic obj, String key) {
    if (obj == null) return null;

    try {
      if (obj is Map) {
        final v = obj[key] ??
            (key == 'lat'
                ? (obj['latitude'] ?? obj['lat'])
                : (obj['longitude'] ?? obj['lng']));

        if (v == null) return null;

        return double.tryParse(v.toString());
      }

      if (key == 'lat') {
        try {
          final v = (obj as dynamic).lat;
          if (v != null) return (v as num).toDouble();
        } catch (_) {}

        try {
          final v = (obj as dynamic).latitude;
          if (v != null) return (v as num).toDouble();
        } catch (_) {}
      } else if (key == 'lng') {
        try {
          final v = (obj as dynamic).lng;
          if (v != null) return (v as num).toDouble();
        } catch (_) {}

        try {
          final v = (obj as dynamic).longitude;
          if (v != null) return (v as num).toDouble();
        } catch (_) {}
      }

      try {
        final map = (obj as dynamic).toJson();

        if (map is Map) {
          final v = map[key] ??
              (key == 'lat' ? map['latitude'] : map['longitude']);

          if (v != null) return double.tryParse(v.toString());
        }
      } catch (_) {}
    } catch (_) {}

    return null;
  }

  List<String> _getSafeList(dynamic obj, String key) {
    if (obj == null) return [];

    try {
      if (obj is Map) {
        final list = obj[key] as List?;
        return list?.map((e) => e.toString()).toList() ?? [];
      }

      if (obj.images != null) {
        return List<String>.from(obj.images);
      }
    } catch (_) {}

    return [];
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> _getAnimalMeta(dynamic obj) {
    if (obj == null) return {};

    try {
      if (obj is Map) {
        final meta = obj['animal_metadata'] ?? obj['animalMetadata'];

        if (meta is Map) return Map<String, dynamic>.from(meta);
      } else {
        final meta = obj.animalMetadata ?? obj.animal_metadata;

        if (meta is Map) return Map<String, dynamic>.from(meta);
      }
    } catch (_) {}

    return {};
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> _getThingMeta(dynamic obj) {
    if (obj == null) return {};

    try {
      if (obj is Map) {
        final meta = obj['thing_metadata'] ?? obj['thingMetadata'];

        if (meta is Map) return Map<String, dynamic>.from(meta);
      } else {
        final meta = obj.thingMetadata ?? obj.thing_metadata;

        if (meta is Map) return Map<String, dynamic>.from(meta);
      }
    } catch (_) {}

    return {};
  }

  bool _getCanEdit(dynamic obj) {
    try {
      if (obj is Map) return obj['permissions']?['can_edit'] == true;

      return obj.permissions.canEdit == true;
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  bool _getCanDelete(dynamic obj) {
    try {
      if (obj is Map) return obj['permissions']?['can_delete'] == true;

      return obj.permissions.canDelete == true;
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  int _getAuthorId(dynamic obj) {
    try {
      if (obj is Map) {
        return int.tryParse(obj['author']?['id'].toString() ?? '0') ?? 0;
      }

      return obj.author.id as int;
    } catch (_) {
      return 0;
    }
  }

  String _getAuthorName(dynamic obj) {
    try {
      if (obj is Map) {
        return obj['author']?['username']?.toString() ?? 'Guest';
      }

      return obj.author.username.toString();
    } catch (_) {
      return 'Guest';
    }
  }

  String? _getAuthorAvatar(dynamic obj) {
    try {
      if (obj is Map) {
        return obj['author']?['avatar_url']?.toString();
      }

      return obj.author.avatarUrl?.toString();
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }
}

// =============================================================================
// Вспомогательный виджет галереи изображений.
// =============================================================================

class _PostDetailsGallery extends StatefulWidget {
  final List<String> images;

  const _PostDetailsGallery({required this.images});

  @override
  State<_PostDetailsGallery> createState() => _PostDetailsGalleryState();
}

class _PostDetailsGalleryState extends State<_PostDetailsGallery> {
  late PageController _pageController;
  int _currentPage = 0;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _pageController = PageController();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _pageController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (widget.images.isEmpty) {
      return Container(
        color: Colors.grey[200],
        child: const Icon(Icons.image_not_supported, size: 50),
      );
    }

    return Stack(
      fit: StackFit.expand,
      children: [
        PageView.builder(
          controller: _pageController,
          itemCount: widget.images.length,
          onPageChanged: (page) => setState(() => _currentPage = page),
          itemBuilder: (context, index) => Image.network(
            widget.images[index],
            fit: BoxFit.cover,
            errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) => Container(
              color: Colors.grey[800],
              child: const Icon(
                Icons.broken_image,
                color: Colors.white54,
                size: 40,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        Positioned(
          bottom: 20,
          left: 0,
          right: 0,
          child: Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
            children: List.generate(
              widget.images.length,
              (index) => Container(
                margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 4),
                height: 4,
                width: index == _currentPage ? 16 : 4,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: index == _currentPage
                      ? const Color(0xFF00F2FF)
                      : Colors.white54,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
