=== FILE: lib/features/comments/presentation/bloc/comments_state.dart ===
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import '../../data/models/comment_model.dart';

abstract class CommentsState extends Equatable {
  const CommentsState();

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [];
}

/// INITIAL
class CommentsInitial extends CommentsState {}

/// LOADING
class CommentsLoading extends CommentsState {}

/// DATA (ТОЛЬКО ДАННЫЕ)
class CommentsLoaded extends CommentsState {
  final List<CommentModel> comments;

  const CommentsLoaded(this.comments);

  CommentsLoaded copyWith({
    List<CommentModel>? comments,
  }) {
    return CommentsLoaded(
      comments ?? this.comments,
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [comments];
}

/// ERROR
class CommentsError extends CommentsState {
  final String message;

  const CommentsError(this.message);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}

/// SUCCESS (под YAML: success + warning)
class CommentActionSuccess extends CommentsState {
  final String? success;
  final String? warning;

  const CommentActionSuccess({
    this.success,
    this.warning,
  });

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [success, warning];
}-e 


=== FILE: lib/features/auth/data/repositories/auth_repository.dart ===
// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/auth/data/repositories/auth_repository.dart
// LAYER: Data
// PURPOSE: Authentication requests to the FindWay Rails backend.
// =============================================================================

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:google_sign_in/google_sign_in.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

class AuthRepository {
  // Dependencies
  final Dio client;
  final SharedPreferences prefs;

  // Key used to store the Rails authentication token.
  static const String _tokenKey = 'auth_token';

  // Google OAuth configuration preserved.
  final GoogleSignIn _googleSignIn = GoogleSignIn(
    serverClientId:
        '620503062302-u4so8o291u7vq3knipnand840rthkgeh.apps.googleusercontent.com',
    scopes: ['email', 'profile'],
  );

  AuthRepository({
    required this.client,
    required this.prefs,
  });

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // REGISTER
  // Rails endpoint: POST /users.json
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Future<String> register(
    String username,
    String email,
    String password,
    String passwordConfirmation,
    bool termsAccepted,
  ) async {
    try {
      final response = await client.post(
        '/users.json',
        data: {
          'user': {
            'username': username.trim(),
            'email': email.trim(),
            'password': password,
            'password_confirmation': passwordConfirmation,
            'terms_accepted': termsAccepted,
          },
        },
      );

      debugPrint(
        '[AuthRepository] Registration response: '
        'status=${response.statusCode}, body=${response.data}',
      );

      await _saveTokenFromResponse(response);

      final data = response.data;

      if (data is Map &&
          data['message'] != null &&
          data['message'].toString().trim().isNotEmpty) {
        return data['message'].toString();
      }

      return 'Реєстрацію завершено. Перевірте електронну пошту.';
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      debugPrint(
        '[AuthRepository] Registration failed: '
        '${e.response?.statusCode} ${e.response?.data}',
      );

      throw Exception(_extractErrorMessage(e));
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('[AuthRepository] Registration failed: $e');
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // LOGIN
  // Rails endpoint: POST /users/sign_in.json
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Future<void> login(String email, String password) async {
    try {
      final response = await client.post(
        '/users/sign_in.json',
        data: {
          'user': {
            'email': email,
            'password': password,
          },
        },
      );

      if (response.statusCode != 200 && response.statusCode != 201) {
        throw Exception('Невірний email або пароль');
      }

      await _saveTokenFromResponse(response);

      debugPrint('[AuthRepository] Email login succeeded');
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      debugPrint(
        '[AuthRepository] Login failed: '
        '${e.response?.statusCode} ${e.response?.data}',
      );

      throw Exception(_extractErrorMessage(e));
    }
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // GOOGLE OAUTH
  // Existing authentication flow preserved.
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Future<void> loginWithGoogle() async {
    try {
      debugPrint('[AuthRepository] Starting Google Sign-In');

      final GoogleSignInAccount? googleUser =
          await _googleSignIn.signIn();

      if (googleUser == null) {
        throw Exception('Вхід скасовано користувачем');
      }

      debugPrint(
        '[AuthRepository] Google account: ${googleUser.email}',
      );

      final GoogleSignInAuthentication googleAuth =
          await googleUser.authentication;

      final String? idToken = googleAuth.idToken;

      if (idToken == null || idToken.isEmpty) {
        throw Exception('Не вдалося отримати idToken від Google');
      }

      final response = await client.post(
        '/api/v1/auth/google',
        data: {'id_token': idToken},
      );

      if (response.statusCode != 200 && response.statusCode != 201) {
        throw Exception(
          'Помилка авторизації на сервері: HTTP ${response.statusCode}',
        );
      }

      // The Google ID token is different from the Rails auth token.
      await _saveTokenFromResponse(response);

      // A Rails token is required for authenticated API requests.
      if (!await hasValidToken()) {
        throw Exception(
          'Rails не повернув токен авторизації',
        );
      }

      debugPrint('[AuthRepository] Google authentication succeeded');
    } catch (e, stackTrace) {
      debugPrint('[AuthRepository] Google authentication failed: $e');
      debugPrintStack(stackTrace: stackTrace);
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // TOKEN MANAGEMENT
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Future<void> _saveTokenFromResponse(Response response) async {
    final dynamic body = response.data;
    String? token;

    // First, check the JSON response body.
    if (body is Map && body['token'] != null) {
      token = body['token'].toString().trim();
    }

    // If the body has no token, check the Authorization header.
    if (token == null || token.isEmpty) {
      final header = response.headers.value('Authorization');

      if (header != null && header.trim().isNotEmpty) {
        token = header
            .replaceFirst(
              RegExp(r'^Bearer\s+', caseSensitive: false),
              '',
            )
            .trim();
      }
    }

    // Store the token only when the server actually returned one.
    if (token != null && token.isNotEmpty) {
      await prefs.setString(_tokenKey, token);
      debugPrint('[AuthRepository] Rails auth token saved');
    }
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // ERROR HANDLING
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

  String _extractErrorMessage(DioException error) {
    final dynamic data = error.response?.data;

    if (data is Map) {
      final dynamic errors = data['errors'];

      if (errors is List && errors.isNotEmpty) {
        return errors.join('\n');
      }

      if (errors is Map) {
        return errors.entries
            .map((entry) => '${entry.value}')
            .join('\n');
      }

      final dynamic message = data['message'];

      if (message != null && message.toString().trim().isNotEmpty) {
        return message.toString();
      }
    }

    if (error.response?.statusCode == 422) {
      return 'Перевірте введені дані. Можливо, email вже зареєстрований '
          'або пароль не відповідає вимогам.';
    }

    if (error.response?.statusCode == 401 ||
        error.response?.statusCode == 403) {
      return 'Невірний email або пароль.';
    }

    if (error.response == null) {
      return 'Не вдалося з’єднатися із сервером. Перевірте інтернет.';
    }

    return 'Помилка сервера: HTTP ${error.response?.statusCode}';
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // LOGOUT SUPPORT
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Future<void> clearToken() async {
    await prefs.remove(_tokenKey);
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // SESSION CHECK
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Future<bool> hasValidToken() async {
    final token = prefs.getString(_tokenKey);
    return token != null && token.trim().isNotEmpty;
  }
}-e

// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/auth/presentation/pages/login_page.dart
// LAYER: Presentation
// PURPOSE: FindWay login and registration screen with Google OAuth.
// =============================================================================
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';

import 'package:findway_mobile/core/terms/terms_checkbox.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/auth/presentation/bloc/auth_bloc.dart';

class LoginPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const LoginPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<LoginPage> createState() => _LoginPageState();
}

class _LoginPageState extends State<LoginPage> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();

  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordConfirmationController = TextEditingController();
  final _usernameController = TextEditingController();

  bool _isSignUp = false;
  bool _obscurePassword = true;
  bool _termsAccepted = false;

  final Color _bgDark = const Color(0xFF0F172A);
  final Color _cardDark = const Color(0xFF1E293B);
  final Color _accentCyan = const Color(0xFF00F2FF);
  final Color _accentOrange = const Color(0xFFFF8A00);

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _emailController.dispose();
    _passwordController.dispose();
    _passwordConfirmationController.dispose();
    _usernameController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _submitForm() {
    if (_isSignUp && !_termsAccepted) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text(
            'Для реєстрації необхідно прийняти умови використання.',
          ),
          backgroundColor: Colors.redAccent,
          behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;

    final email = _emailController.text.trim();
    final password = _passwordController.text;

    if (_isSignUp) {
      final username = _usernameController.text.trim();
      final confirmation = _passwordConfirmationController.text;

      if (password != confirmation) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(
            content: Text('Паролі не співпадають'),
            backgroundColor: Colors.redAccent,
            behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
          ),
        );
        return;
      }

      context.read<AuthBloc>().add(
            RegisterSubmitted(
              username: username,
              email: email,
              password: password,
              passwordConfirmation: confirmation,
              termsAccepted: _termsAccepted,
            ),
          );
    } else {
      context.read<AuthBloc>().add(
            LoginSubmitted(email, password),
          );
    }
  }

  void _submitGoogleLogin() {
    debugPrint('>>> [UI] Google Sign-In button clicked');
    context.read<AuthBloc>().add(GoogleLoginSubmitted());
  }

  void _toggleAuthMode() {
    setState(() {
      _isSignUp = !_isSignUp;
      _termsAccepted = false;
      _formKey.currentState?.reset();
    });
  }

  void _showRegistrationSuccess(String message) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
      ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
      ..showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text(
            '$message\nПеревірте пошту та підтвердьте акаунт.',
          ),
          backgroundColor: Colors.green.shade700,
          behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
          duration: const Duration(seconds: 6),
        ),
      );

    setState(() {
      _isSignUp = false;
      _termsAccepted = false;
      _formKey.currentState?.reset();
    });

    _passwordController.clear();
    _passwordConfirmationController.clear();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: _bgDark,
      body: SafeArea(
      child: BlocConsumer<AuthBloc, AuthState>(
        listener: (context, state) {
          debugPrint('[LoginPage] STATE: ${state.runtimeType}');

          if (state is AuthRegistrationSuccess) {
            debugPrint('[LoginPage] REGISTRATION SUCCESS');

            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
              ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
              ..showSnackBar(
                SnackBar(
                  content: Text(
                    '${state.message}\n'
                    'Перевірте пошту та підтвердьте акаунт.',
                  ),
                  duration: const Duration(seconds: 8),
                  behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
                  backgroundColor: Colors.green,
                ),
              );

            setState(() {
              _isSignUp = false;
              _termsAccepted = false;
            });

            _passwordController.clear();
            _passwordConfirmationController.clear();
          } else if (state is AuthError) {
            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
              SnackBar(
                content: Text(state.message),
                backgroundColor: Colors.redAccent,
              ),
            );
          }
        },
        builder: (context, state) {
  
            final isLoading = state is AuthLoading;

            return Center(
              child: SingleChildScrollView(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                  horizontal: 24,
                  vertical: 16,
                ),
                child: Form(
                  key: _formKey,
                  child: Column(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                    children: [
                      Center(
                        child: Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(4),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            shape: BoxShape.circle,
                            color: _cardDark,
                            boxShadow: [
                              BoxShadow(
                                color: _accentCyan.withValues(alpha: 0.35),
                                blurRadius: 24,
                                spreadRadius: 3,
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                          child: ClipOval(
                            child: Image.asset(
                              'assets/images/findway_logo.png',
                              height: 80,
                              width: 80,
                              fit: BoxFit.cover,
                              errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) {
                                return Image.asset(
                                  'assets/images/logo.png',
                                  height: 80,
                                  width: 80,
                                  fit: BoxFit.cover,
                                  errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) {
                                    return Container(
                                      width: 80,
                                      height: 80,
                                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                        color: _accentCyan.withValues(
                                          alpha: 0.1,
                                        ),
                                        shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                        border: Border.all(
                                          color: _accentCyan,
                                          width: 2,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                      child: Icon(
                                        Icons.radar_rounded,
                                        size: 44,
                                        color: _accentCyan,
                                      ),
                                    );
                                  },
                                );
                              },
                            ),
                          ),

                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      Text(

                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,

                        TextFormField(
                          controller: _usernameController,
                          textInputAction: TextInputAction.next,
                          style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
                          decoration: _inputDecoration(
                            "Ім'я користувача",
                            Icons.person_outline,
                          ),
                          validator: (value) {
                            if (!_isSignUp) return null;

                            if (value == null || value.trim().isEmpty) {
                              return "Введіть ім'я";
                            }

                            return null;
                          },

                        const SizedBox(height: 14),

                          'Email',

                            return 'Введіть коректний email';

                        },

                        decoration: _inputDecoration(

                          Icons.lock_outline,

                                  ? Icons.visibility_off

                              color: Colors.white54,
                            ),
                            onPressed: () {
                              setState(() {
                                _obscurePassword = !_obscurePassword;
                              });
                            },
                          ),
                        ),
                        validator: (value) {
                          if (value == null || value.length < 6) {
                            return 'Пароль має бути від 6 символів';

                        },
                      ),

                      if (_isSignUp) ...[

                          textInputAction: TextInputAction.done,

                            Icons.lock_outline,

                            if (!_isSignUp) return null;

                            if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
                              return 'Підтвердіть пароль';
                            }

                            if (value != _passwordController.text) {
                              return 'Паролі не співпадають';
                            }

                            return null;
                          },
                          onFieldSubmitted: (_) {
                            if (!isLoading) _submitForm();
                          },
                        ),
                      ],

                                width: 20,
                                child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                                  color: Colors.black,
                                  strokeWidth: 2,
                                ),

                                    ? 'ЗАРЕЄСТРУВАТИСЯ'

  }

  InputDecoration _inputDecoration(String label, IconData icon) {
    return InputDecoration(
      labelText: label,
      labelStyle: const TextStyle(
        color: Colors.white54,

class _GoogleGLogo extends StatelessWidget {
  final double size;

  const _GoogleGLogo({this.size = 24});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Image.asset(
      'assets/images/google_logo.png',
      width: size,
      height: size,
      errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) {
        return Image.asset(
          'assets/google.png',
          width: size,
          height: size,
          errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) {
            return CustomPaint(
              size: Size(size, size),
              painter: _GoogleGLogoPainter(),
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

class _GoogleGLogoPainter extends CustomPainter {
  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final double center = size.width / 2;
    final double radius = size.width / 2;

    final Rect rect = Rect.fromCircle(
      center: Offset(center, center),
      radius: radius - 2,
    );

    final double strokeWidth = size.width * 0.22;

    final Paint redPaint = Paint()
      ..color = const Color(0xFFEA4335)
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = strokeWidth
      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.butt;

    final Paint yellowPaint = Paint()
      ..color = const Color(0xFFFBBC05)
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = strokeWidth
      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.butt;

    final Paint greenPaint = Paint()
      ..color = const Color(0xFF34A853)
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = strokeWidth
      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.butt;

    final Paint bluePaint = Paint()
      ..color = const Color(0xFF4285F4)
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = strokeWidth
      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.butt;

    final Paint blueFill = Paint()
      ..color = const Color(0xFF4285F4)
      ..style = PaintingStyle.fill;

    canvas.drawArc(rect, -0.8, 1.8, false, redPaint);
    canvas.drawArc(rect, 1.0, 1.1, false, yellowPaint);
    canvas.drawArc(rect, 2.1, 1.1, false, greenPaint);
    canvas.drawArc(rect, -0.3, 0.9, false, bluePaint);

    canvas.drawRect(
      Rect.fromLTWH(
        center - 1,
        center - strokeWidth / 2,
        radius * 0.9,
        strokeWidth,
      ),
      blueFill,
    );
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant CustomPainter oldDelegate) => false;
}-e 


=== FILE: lib/features/auth/presentation/bloc/auth_bloc.dart ===
// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/features/auth/presentation/bloc/auth_bloc.dart
// LAYER: Presentation (BLoC)
// RESPONSIBILITY: Manages application authentication state transitions.
// =============================================================================
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/auth/data/repositories/auth_repository.dart';

// =============================================================================
// EVENTS
// =============================================================================

abstract class AuthEvent extends Equatable {
  const AuthEvent();

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [];
}

class CheckAuthStatus extends AuthEvent {}

class LoginSubmitted extends AuthEvent {
  final String email;
  final String password;

  const LoginSubmitted(this.email, this.password);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [email, password];
}

class RegisterSubmitted extends AuthEvent {
  final String username;
  final String email;
  final String password;
  final String passwordConfirmation;
  final bool termsAccepted;

  const RegisterSubmitted({
    required this.username,
    required this.email,
    required this.password,
    required this.passwordConfirmation,
    required this.termsAccepted,
  });

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
        username,
        email,
        password,
        passwordConfirmation,
        termsAccepted,
      ];
}

class GoogleLoginSubmitted extends AuthEvent {}

class LogoutRequested extends AuthEvent {}

// =============================================================================
// STATES
// =============================================================================

abstract class AuthState extends Equatable {
  const AuthState();

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [];
}

class AuthInitial extends AuthState {}

class AuthLoading extends AuthState {}

class AuthAuthenticated extends AuthState {}

class AuthRegistrationSuccess extends AuthState {
  final String message;

  const AuthRegistrationSuccess(this.message);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}

class AuthError extends AuthState {
  final String message;

  const AuthError(this.message);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}

// =============================================================================
// BLOC
// =============================================================================

class AuthBloc extends Bloc<AuthEvent, AuthState> {
  final AuthRepository repository;

  AuthBloc({required this.repository}) : super(AuthInitial()) {
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Check authentication status.
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    on<CheckAuthStatus>((event, emit) async {
      emit(AuthLoading());

      try {
        // Existing session-check behavior preserved.
        emit(AuthAuthenticated());
      } catch (_) {
        emit(AuthInitial());
      }
    });

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Login with email and password.
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    on<LoginSubmitted>((event, emit) async {
      emit(AuthLoading());

      try {
        await repository.login(event.email, event.password);
        emit(AuthAuthenticated());
      } catch (e) {
        emit(AuthError(_errorMessage(e)));
      }
    });

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Register with username, email, password and terms acceptance.
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    on<RegisterSubmitted>((event, emit) async {
      emit(AuthLoading());

      try {
        final message = await repository.register(
          event.username,
          event.email,
          event.password,
          event.passwordConfirmation,
          event.termsAccepted,
        );
        debugPrint('[AuthBloc] Registration succeeded: $message');
        emit(AuthRegistrationSuccess(message));
      } catch (e) {
        emit(
          AuthError(
            e.toString().replaceFirst('Exception: ', ''),
          ),
        );
      }
    });

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Google OAuth — existing behavior preserved.
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    on<GoogleLoginSubmitted>((event, emit) async {
      emit(AuthLoading());

      try {
        await repository.loginWithGoogle();
        emit(AuthAuthenticated());
      } catch (e) {
        emit(AuthError(_errorMessage(e)));
      }
    });

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Logout.
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    on<LogoutRequested>((event, emit) async {
      try {
        await repository.client.delete('/users/sign_out.json');
      } catch (_) {
        // Ignore network errors during logout.
      } finally {
        emit(AuthInitial());
      }
    });
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Error message formatting.
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

  String _errorMessage(Object error) {
    return error.toString().replaceFirst('Exception: ', '');
  }
}-e 


=== FILE: lib/core/network/auth_interceptor.dart ===
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

class AuthInterceptor extends Interceptor {
  final SharedPreferences prefs;
  final void Function()? onUnauthenticated;

  static const String _tokenKey = 'auth_token';

  AuthInterceptor({
    required this.prefs,
    this.onUnauthenticated,
  });

  @override
  void onRequest(
    RequestOptions options,
    RequestInterceptorHandler handler,
  ) {
    final isGoogleAuthRequest = options.path.contains('/api/v1/auth/google');
    final token = prefs.getString(_tokenKey);

    if (!isGoogleAuthRequest && token != null && token.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      options.headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer ${token.trim()}';
    }

    handler.next(options);
  }

  @override
  void onResponse(
    Response response,
    ResponseInterceptorHandler handler,
  ) {
    // Так как DioClient пробивает 401 через validateStatus < 500, обрабатываем 401 здесь
    if (response.statusCode == 401) {
      _processUnauthenticated(response.requestOptions.path);
    }

    handler.next(response);
  }

  @override
  void onError(
    DioException err,
    ErrorInterceptorHandler handler,
  ) {
    if (err.response?.statusCode == 401) {
      _processUnauthenticated(err.requestOptions.path);
    }

    handler.next(err);
  }

  /// Проверяет, является ли 401 критической ошибкой сессии
  void _processUnauthenticated(String path) {
    // НЕ выбиваем пользователя из приложения при ошибках загрузки контента (статей, биометрии)
    // Разлогин вызываем только при отказе системных эндпоинтов авторизации/профиля
    final isContentEndpoint = path.contains('/articles') || 
                              path.contains('/biometrics') || 
                              path.contains('/posts') ||
                              path.contains('/matches');

    if (!isContentEndpoint) {
      onUnauthenticated?.call();
    }
  }
}-e 


=== FILE: lib/core/network/dio_client.dart ===
import 'dart:io';
import 'package:cookie_jar/cookie_jar.dart';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:dio_cookie_manager/dio_cookie_manager.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

import 'auth_interceptor.dart';

class DioClient {
  final Dio _dio;

  static final CookieJar _cookieJar = CookieJar();

  DioClient(
    this._dio, {
    required SharedPreferences prefs,
    void Function()? onUnauthenticated,
    String? customBaseUrl,
  }) {
    // 💡 Определяем корректный URL в зависимости от среды
    _dio.options.baseUrl = customBaseUrl ?? _resolveBaseUrl();
    _dio.options.connectTimeout = const Duration(seconds: 15);
    _dio.options.receiveTimeout = const Duration(seconds: 15);

    _dio.options.headers = {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      'Accept': 'application/json',
    };

    _dio.options.validateStatus = (status) => status != null && status < 500;

    _dio.interceptors.add(
      CookieManager(_cookieJar),
    );

    _dio.interceptors.add(
      AuthInterceptor(
        prefs: prefs,
        onUnauthenticated: onUnauthenticated,
      ),
    );

    _dio.interceptors.add(
      LogInterceptor(
        requestHeader: false,
        requestBody: false,
        responseHeader: false,
        responseBody: false,
      ),
    );
  }

  static String _resolveBaseUrl() {
    if (kReleaseMode) {
      return 'https://findway.pro';
    }
    // Для реального Android-устройства с adb reverse tcp:3000 tcp:3000
    if (!kIsWeb && Platform.isAndroid) {
      return 'http://localhost:3000';
    }
    return 'http://127.0.0.1:3000';
  }

  Future<Response> get(
    String url, {
    Map<String, dynamic>? queryParameters,
  }) async {
    return _dio.get(
      url,
      queryParameters: queryParameters,
    );
  }

  Future<Response> post(
    String url, {
    dynamic data,
    Map<String, dynamic>? queryParameters,
  }) async {
    return _dio.post(
      url,
      data: data,
      queryParameters: queryParameters,
    );
  }

  Future<Response> delete(String url) async {
    return _dio.delete(url);
  }

  Future<Response> patch(
    String url, {
    dynamic data,
  }) async {
    return _dio.patch(
      url,
      data: data,
    );
  }
}-e 


=== FILE: lib/core/terms/terms_repository.dart ===
// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/core/terms/terms_repository.dart
// PURPOSE: Load and cache localized legal documents from JSON assets.
// =============================================================================

import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:flutter/services.dart';

import 'data/terms_document.dart';

class TermsRepository {
  TermsRepository._();

  static final TermsRepository instance = TermsRepository._();

  static const Map<String, String> _assetPaths = {
    'UA:uk': 'assets/terms/UA.json',
  };

  final Map<String, TermsDocument> _cache = {};

  /// Loads a legal document for the specified country and locale.
  ///
  /// Example:
  /// final document = await TermsRepository.instance.load(
  ///   country: 'UA',
  ///   locale: 'uk',
  /// );
  Future<TermsDocument> load({
    required String country,
    required String locale,
  }) async {
    final key = '${country.toUpperCase()}:${locale.toLowerCase()}';

    final cachedDocument = _cache[key];
    if (cachedDocument != null) {
      return cachedDocument;
    }

    final assetPath = _assetPaths[key];

    if (assetPath == null) {
      throw UnsupportedError(
        'Legal document is not configured for $key.',
      );
    }

    final jsonString = await rootBundle.loadString(assetPath);

    final decoded = jsonDecode(jsonString);

    if (decoded is! Map<String, dynamic>) {
      throw const FormatException(
        'Legal document JSON must contain an object.',
      );
    }

    final document = TermsDocument.fromJson(decoded);

    if (document.country.toUpperCase() != country.toUpperCase() ||
        document.locale.toLowerCase() != locale.toLowerCase()) {
      throw const FormatException(
        'Legal document country or locale does not match the requested '
        'configuration.',
      );
    }

    _cache[key] = document;

    return document;
  }

  /// Clears the cached document, useful after updating a local asset
  /// or in tests.
  void clearCache() {
    _cache.clear();
  }
}-e

// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/core/terms/terms_checkbox.dart
// PURPOSE: Localized terms acceptance checkbox and document dialog.
// =============================================================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:url_launcher/url_launcher.dart';

import 'data/terms_document.dart';
import 'terms_dialog.dart';
import 'terms_repository.dart';

class TermsCheckbox extends StatefulWidget {
  final bool value;
  final ValueChanged<bool> onChanged;
  final bool enabled;

  final String country;
  final String locale;

  const TermsCheckbox({
    super.key,
    required this.value,
    required this.onChanged,
    this.enabled = true,
    this.country = 'UA',
    this.locale = 'uk',
  });

  static const Color _accent = Color(0xFF00F2FF);

  @override
  State<TermsCheckbox> createState() => _TermsCheckboxState();
}

class _TermsCheckboxState extends State<TermsCheckbox> {
  TermsDocument? _document;
  Object? _loadError;
  bool _isLoading = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadDocument();
  }

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant TermsCheckbox oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);

    if (oldWidget.country != widget.country ||
        oldWidget.locale != widget.locale) {
      _loadDocument();
    }
  }

  Future<void> _loadDocument() async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _loadError = null;
    });

    try {
      final document = await TermsRepository.instance.load(
        country: widget.country,
        locale: widget.locale,
      );

      if (!mounted) return;

      setState(() {
        _document = document;
        _isLoading = false;
      });
    } catch (error) {
      if (!mounted) return;

      setState(() {
        _loadError = error;
        _isLoading = false;
      });

      debugPrint('Failed to load legal document: $error');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _openFullDocument(TermsDocument document) async {
    final uri = Uri.tryParse(document.fullVersion.url);

    if (uri == null ||
        (uri.scheme != 'https' && uri.scheme != 'http') ||
        uri.host.isEmpty) {
      _showError('Некоректне посилання на повний текст документа.');
      return;
    }

    final opened = await launchUrl(
      uri,
      mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication,
    );

    if (!opened && mounted) {
      _showError('Не вдалося відкрити повний текст документа.');
    }
  }

  void _showError(String message) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: Text(message),
        behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _showTerms() async {
    final document = _document;
    if (document == null || !widget.enabled) return;

    await TermsDialog.show(
      context,
      document: document,
      onOpenFullDocument: () => _openFullDocument(document),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_isLoading) {
      return const Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            SizedBox(
              width: 18,
              height: 18,
              child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2),
            ),
            SizedBox(width: 12),
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                'Завантаження умов використання...',
                style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 12),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }

    if (_loadError != null || _document == null) {
      return Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            const Expanded(
              child: Text(
                'Не вдалося завантажити умови використання.',
                style: TextStyle(color: Colors.redAccent, fontSize: 12),
              ),
            ),
            TextButton(
              onPressed: widget.enabled ? _loadDocument : null,
              child: const Text('Повторити'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }

    final document = _document!;

    return CheckboxListTile(
      value: widget.value,
      onChanged: widget.enabled
          ? (checked) => widget.onChanged(checked ?? false)
          : null,
      controlAffinity: ListTileControlAffinity.leading,
      contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
      activeColor: TermsCheckbox._accent,
      checkColor: const Color(0xFF0F172A),
      title: Wrap(
        crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
        children: [
          Text(
            document.acceptance.checkboxLabel,
            style: const TextStyle(
              color: Colors.white70,
              fontSize: 12,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 4),
          GestureDetector(
            onTap: widget.enabled ? _showTerms : null,
            child: Text(
              document.fullVersion.label,
              style: const TextStyle(
                color: TermsCheckbox._accent,
                fontSize: 12,
                decoration: TextDecoration.underline,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}-e 


=== FILE: lib/core/terms/data/terms_document.dart ===
// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/core/terms/data/terms_document.dart
// PURPOSE: Common data models for localized legal documents.
// =============================================================================

class TermsDocument {
  final String country;
  final String locale;
  final String documentType;
  final String version;

  final String title;
  final String subtitle;
  final String closeButton;

  final TermsFullVersion fullVersion;
  final TermsAcceptance acceptance;
  final List<TermsSection> sections;

  const TermsDocument({
    required this.country,
    required this.locale,
    required this.documentType,
    required this.version,
    required this.title,
    required this.subtitle,
    required this.closeButton,
    required this.fullVersion,
    required this.acceptance,
    required this.sections,
  });

  factory TermsDocument.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return TermsDocument(
      country: json['country'] as String,
      locale: json['locale'] as String,
      documentType: json['document_type'] as String,
      version: json['version'] as String,
      title: json['title'] as String,
      subtitle: json['subtitle'] as String,
      closeButton: json['close_button'] as String,
      fullVersion: TermsFullVersion.fromJson(
        json['full_version'] as Map<String, dynamic>,
      ),
      acceptance: TermsAcceptance.fromJson(
        json['acceptance'] as Map<String, dynamic>,
      ),
      sections: (json['sections'] as List<dynamic>)
          .map(
            (section) => TermsSection.fromJson(
              section as Map<String, dynamic>,
            ),
          )
          .toList(),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'country': country,
      'locale': locale,
      'document_type': documentType,
      'version': version,
      'title': title,
      'subtitle': subtitle,
      'close_button': closeButton,
      'full_version': fullVersion.toJson(),
      'acceptance': acceptance.toJson(),
      'sections': sections.map((section) => section.toJson()).toList(),
    };
  }
}

class TermsFullVersion {
  final String label;
  final String url;

  const TermsFullVersion({
    required this.label,
    required this.url,
  });

  factory TermsFullVersion.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return TermsFullVersion(
      label: json['label'] as String,
      url: json['url'] as String,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'label': label,
      'url': url,
    };
  }
}

class TermsAcceptance {
  final String checkboxLabel;
  final String validationError;

  const TermsAcceptance({
    required this.checkboxLabel,
    required this.validationError,
  });

  factory TermsAcceptance.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return TermsAcceptance(
      checkboxLabel: json['checkbox_label'] as String,
      validationError: json['validation_error'] as String,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'checkbox_label': checkboxLabel,
      'validation_error': validationError,
    };
  }
}

class TermsSection {
  final String id;
  final String title;
  final String content;
  final bool required;

  const TermsSection({
    required this.id,
    required this.title,
    required this.content,
    this.required = false,
  });

  factory TermsSection.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return TermsSection(
      id: json['id'] as String,
      title: json['title'] as String,
      content: json['content'] as String,
      required: json['required'] as bool? ?? false,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'title': title,
      'content': content,
      'required': required,
    };
  }
}-e

// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/core/terms/terms_dialog.dart
// PURPOSE: Display a localized legal document loaded from JSON.

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import 'data/terms_document.dart';

class TermsDialog extends StatelessWidget {
  final TermsDocument document;
  final VoidCallback? onOpenFullDocument;

  const TermsDialog({
    super.key,
    required this.document,
    this.onOpenFullDocument,
  });

  static const Color _surface = Color(0xFF1E293B);
  static const Color _accent = Color(0xFF00F2FF);
  static const Color _secondary = Color(0xFFFF8A00);

  static Future<void> show(
    BuildContext context, {
    required TermsDocument document,
    VoidCallback? onOpenFullDocument,
  }) {
    return showDialog<void>(
      context: context,
      builder: (_) => TermsDialog(
        document: document,
        onOpenFullDocument: onOpenFullDocument,
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AlertDialog(
      backgroundColor: _surface,
      titlePadding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 24, 24, 8),
      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 12, 24, 8),
      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        side: BorderSide(
          color: _accent.withValues(alpha: 0.25),
        ),
      ),
      title: Text(
        document.title,
        style: const TextStyle(
          color: _accent,
          fontSize: 18,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
        ),
      ),
      content: SizedBox(
        width: 520,
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              if (document.subtitle.isNotEmpty) ...[
                Text(
                  document.subtitle,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    color: Colors.white70,
                    fontSize: 13,
                    height: 1.5,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
              ],
              for (final section in document.sections) ...[
                Text(
                  section.title,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    color: _secondary,
                    fontSize: 15,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(
                  section.content,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    color: Colors.white70,
                    fontSize: 14,
                    height: 1.5,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
              ],
              if (onOpenFullDocument != null) ...[
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                SizedBox(
                  width: double.infinity,
                  child: OutlinedButton(
                    onPressed: onOpenFullDocument,
                    style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                      foregroundColor: _accent,
                      side: BorderSide(
                        color: _accent.withValues(alpha: 0.5),
                      ),
                      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                      ),
                    ),
                    child: Text(document.fullVersion.label),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
      actions: [
        TextButton(
          onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
          child: Text(
            document.closeButton,
            style: const TextStyle(color: _accent),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

// =============================================================================
// FILE: lib/main.dart
// LAYER: Application Root & Navigation
// RESPONSIBILITY: Global dependency initialization, MultiBlocProvider setup,
//                 and reactive auth-state routing (Production Guard).
// =============================================================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'injection_container.dart' as di;

// --- СТРАНИЦЫ (PAGES) ---
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/pages/search_page.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/auth/presentation/pages/login_page.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/profile/presentation/pages/profile_page.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/pages/post_card_page.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/pages/post_edit_page.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/chat/presentation/pages/chat_room_page.dart';

// --- БЛОКИ И СОБЫТИЯ (BLOCS & EVENTS) ---
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/auth/presentation/bloc/auth_bloc.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/profile/presentation/bloc/profile_bloc.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/profile/presentation/bloc/profile_event.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/bloc/search_bloc.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/search/presentation/bloc/search_event.dart';
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/chat/presentation/bloc/chat_bloc.dart'; // 👉 ДОБАВЛЕН
import 'package:findway_mobile/features/chat/presentation/bloc/notification_bloc.dart';

void main() async {
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
  
  // Инициализация сервисов и инжектора зависимостей (GetIt)
  await di.init();
  
  runApp(const FindWayApp());
}

class FindWayApp extends StatelessWidget {
  const FindWayApp({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return MultiBlocProvider(
      providers: [
        // --- ГЛОБАЛЬНЫЕ БЛОКИ ---
        BlocProvider<AuthBloc>(
          create: (context) => di.sl<AuthBloc>(),
        ),
        BlocProvider<NotificationBloc>(
          create: (context) => di.sl<NotificationBloc>(),
        ),
      ],
      child: MaterialApp(
        debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
        title: 'FindWay',
        theme: ThemeData(
          useMaterial3: true,
          colorSchemeSeed: const Color(0xFF00F2FF),
          fontFamily: 'Orbitron',
        ),

        // =====================================================================
        // 🔥 АВТОМАТИЧЕСКИЙ РЕАКТИВНЫЙ РОУТИНГ (PRODUCTION GUARD)
        // =====================================================================
        home: BlocBuilder<AuthBloc, AuthState>(
          builder: (context, state) {
            if (state is AuthAuthenticated) {
              return const SearchPage();
            }

            // LoginPage остаётся смонтированным и сохраняет BlocConsumer.
            return const LoginPage();
          },
        ),

        // --- ИМЕНОВАННЫЕ МАРШРУТЫ ---
        onGenerateRoute: (settings) {
          switch (settings.name) {
            case '/login':
              return MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const LoginPage());

            case '/':
              return MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const SearchPage());

            // --- КАРТОЧКА ПОСТА ---
            case '/post_details':
              final args = settings.arguments as Map<String, dynamic>?;
              final postId = args?['id'] as int? ?? 0;
              final category = args?['category'] as String? ?? 'people';
              
              return MaterialPageRoute(
                builder: (_) => BlocProvider<SearchBloc>(
                  create: (context) => di.sl<SearchBloc>()..add(LoadPostDetails(
                    id: postId,
                    category: category,
                    locale: 'uk', 
                  )),
                  child: const PostCardPage(), 
                ),
              );

            // --- РЕДАКТИРОВАНИЕ ПОСТА ---
            case '/post_edit':
              final args = settings.arguments as Map<String, dynamic>;
              return MaterialPageRoute(
                builder: (_) => BlocProvider<SearchBloc>.value(
                  value: args['bloc'] as SearchBloc, 
                  child: PostEditPage(
                    postId: args['postId'],
                    initialCategory: args['initialCategory'],
                    initialTitle: args['initialTitle'],
                    initialText: args['initialText'],
                    initialLocalId: args['initialLocalId'],
                    initialChoiceId: args['initialChoiceId'],
                    initialActionId: args['initialActionId'],
                    existingImages: args['existingImages'] as List<String>? ?? [],
                  ),
                ),
              );

            // --- ЛИЧНЫЕ СООБЩЕНИЯ (ЧАТ) ---
            case '/chat':
              final args = settings.arguments as Map<String, dynamic>;
              return MaterialPageRoute(
                builder: (_) => BlocProvider<ChatBloc>(
                  create: (context) => di.sl<ChatBloc>(), 
                  child: ChatRoomPage(
                    recipientId: args['recipientId'] as int,
                    recipientName: args['username'] as String,
                    avatarUrl: args['avatarUrl'] as String?,
                    currentLocale: args['currentLocale'] as String,
                  ),
                ),
              );

            // --- ЛИЧНЫЙ КАБИНЕТ (ПРОФИЛЬ) ---
            case '/profile':
              return MaterialPageRoute(
                builder: (_) => BlocProvider<ProfileBloc>(
                  create: (context) => di.sl<ProfileBloc>()..add(GetProfileData('uk')), 
                  child: const ProfilePage(currentLocale: 'uk'),
                ),
              );

            default:
              return MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const LoginPage());
          }
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}-e
